Manusmṛti
Typed, analyzed and proofread by M-YANO and Y-IKARI 
(May-June 1991, January-April 1992, March-April 1996)

 
K: Manusmṛti with the Sanskrit Commentary Manvartha-Muktāvalī of Kullūka 
Bhaṭṭa, ed- J-L-Shastri 1983-  (Compared with the edition of 
Kashi Skt Series 114 ed- Haragovinda Sastri-)
M: Manusmṛti with the commentary of Medātithi, 2vols- Calcutta 1967
M: Manu-smṛti with the "Manubhāṣya" of Medhātithi, ed- :na:ngānātha ;nhā, 
GOI 1932,1939 rep- 1992	
 
Text is based upon K, and M's variant is given at each pada-end-
There are verses which are found only in K or M- The difference of
the ;sloka-numbering of chapter between K and M is noticed- 
 
Some sandhis have been dissolved and word division marks such as
"-" and "-" are introduced in the text in order to have easy 
identification of individual word form-
"-" indicates resolve of the external sandhi-
"-" indicates word division within a compound-

[M:] variants of Medhātithi's 
Different numbering of verses between M and K is noted-

<B 1>

Manu01-01a/ manum ekāgram āsīnam abhigamya) maharṣayaḥ |
Manu01-01c/ pratipūjya) yathānyāyam idaṃ vacanam abruvan) ‖
Manu01-02a/ bhagavan sarvavarṇānāṃ yathāvad anupūrva;saḥ |
Manu01-02c/ antaraprabhavānāṃ ca dharmān no vaktum arhasi) ‖
Manu01-03a/ tvam eko hy asya sarvasya vidhānasya svayaṃbhuvaḥ |
Manu01-03c/ acintyasya-aprameyasya kāryatattvārthavit prabho ‖
Manu01-04a/ sa taiḥ pṛṣṭas) tathā samyag amita-ojā mahātmabhiḥ |
Manu01-04c/ pratyuvāca)-arcya) tān sarvān maharṣīn- ;srūyatām) iti ‖
Manu01-05a/ āsīd) idam tamobhūtam a-praj;nātam a-lakṣaṇam |
Manu01-05c/ a-pratarkyam a-vij;neyaṃ prasuptam iva sarvataḥ ‖
Manu01-06a/ tataḥ svayaṃbhūr bhagavān avyakto vya;njayann) idam |
Manu01-06c/ mahābhūtādi vṛtta-ojāḥ prādur āsīt) tamonudaḥ ‖
Manu01-07a/ yo 'asāv atīndriyagrāhyaḥ sūkṣmo 'avyaktaḥ sanātanaḥ |
Manu01-07c/ sarvabhūtamayo 'acintyaḥ sa eva svayam udbabhau) ‖| [ṃ- sa eṣa]
Manu01-08a/ so 'abhidhyāya) ;sarīrāt svāt sisṛkṣur vividhāḥ prajāḥ |
Manu01-08c/ apa eva sasarja)-ādau tāsu vīryam avāsṛjat) ‖
Manu01-09a/ tad aṇḍam abhavadd) haimaṃ sahasrāṃ;susama-prabham | 
Manu01-09c/ tasmi;n jaj;ne) svayaṃ brahmā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ ‖
Manu01-10a/ āpo narā iti proktā) āpo vai narasūnavaḥ |
Manu01-10c/ tā yad asyāyanaṃ pūrvaṃ tena nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ‖
Manu01-11a/ yat tat kāraṇam avyaktaṃ nityaṃ sad-asad-ātmakaṃ |
Manu01-11c/ tad-visṛṣṭaḥ sa puruṣo loke brahmā-iti kīrtyate) ‖

Manu01-12a/ tasminn aṇḍe sa bhagavān uṣitvā) parivatsaram |
Manu01-12c/ svayam evātmano dhyānāt tad aṇḍam akarod) dvidhā ‖
Manu01-13a/ tābhyāṃ sa ;sakalābhyāṃ ca divaṃ bhūmiṃ ca nirmame) |
Manu01-13c/ madhye vyoma di;sa;s ca-aṣṭāv apāṃ sthānaṃ ca ;sā;svataṃ ‖
Manu01-14a/ udbabarha)-ātmana;s ca-eva manaḥ sad-asad-ātmakam |
Manu01-14c/ manasa;s ca-apy ahaṃkāram abhimantāram ī;svaram ‖ [ṃ- aha:nkāram]
Manu01-15a/ mahāntam eva ca-ātmānaṃ sarvāṇi tri-guṇāni ca |
Manu01-15c/ viṣayāṇāṃ grahītṝṇi ;sanaiḥ pa;nca-indriyāṇi ca ‖
Manu01-16a/ teṣāṃ tv avayavān sūkṣmān ṣaṇṇām apy amita-ojasām |
Manu01-16c/ saṃnive;sya)-ātmamātrāsu sarvabhūtāni nirmame) ‖ [ṃ- sannive;sya]
Manu01-17a/ yan mūrti-avayavāḥ sūkṣmās tāni-imāny ā;srayanti) ṣaṭ |
Manu01-17c/ tasmāt-;sarīram ity āhus) tasya mūrtiṃ manīṣiṇaḥ ‖
Manu01-18a/ tad āvi;santi) bhūtāni mahānti saha karmabhiḥ |
Manu01-18c/ mana;s ca-avayavaiḥ sūkṣmaiḥ sarvabhūtakṛd avyayam ‖
Manu01-19a/ teṣām idaṃ tu saptānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ mahā-ojasām |
Manu01-19c/ sūkṣmābhyo mūrtimātrābhyaḥ saṃbhavaty) avyayād vyayam ‖
Manu01-20a/ ādyādyasya guṇaṃ tv eṣām avāpnoti) paraḥ paraḥ |
Manu01-20c/ yo yo yāvatitha;s ca-eṣāṃ sa sa tāvad guṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ‖
Manu01-21a/ sarveṣāṃ tu sa nāmāni karmāṇi ca pṛthak pṛthak |
Manu01-21c/ veda;sabdebhya eva-ādau pṛthak saṃsthā;s ca nirmame) ‖
Manu01-22a/ karmātmanāṃ ca devānāṃ so 'asṛjat) prāṇināṃ prabhuḥ |
Manu01-22c/ sādhyānāṃ ca gaṇaṃ sūkṣmaṃ yaj;naṃ ca-eva sanātanam ‖
Manu01-23a/ agni-vāyu-ravibhyas tu trayaṃ brahma sanātanam |
Manu01-23c/ dudoha) yaj;nasiddhi-artham ṛc-yajus-sāma-lakṣaṇam ‖
Manu01-24a/ kālaṃ kālavibhaktī;s ca nakṣatrāṇi grahāṃs tathā |
Manu01-24c/ saritaḥ sāgarān-;sailān samāni viṣamāni ca ‖
Manu01-25a/ tapo vācaṃ ratiṃ ca-eva kāmaṃ ca krodham eva ca |
Manu01-25c/ sṛṣṭiṃ sasarja ca-eva-imāṃ sraṣṭum icchann) imāḥ prajāḥ ‖
Manu01-26a/ karmaṇāṃ ca vivekārthaṃ dharma-adharmau vyavecayat) | [K:vivekāya ]
Manu01-26c/ dvandvair ayojayac) ca-imāḥ sukha-duḥkhādibhiḥ prajāḥ ‖
Manu01-27a/ aṇvyo mātrā vinā;sinyo da;sārdhānāṃ tu yāḥ smṛtāḥ |
Manu01-27c/ tābhiḥ sārdham idaṃ sarvaṃ saṃbhavaty) anupūrva;saḥ ‖
Manu01-28a/ yaṃ tu karmaṇi yasmin sa nyayu:nkta) prathamaṃ prabhuḥ |
Manu01-28c/ sa tad eva svayaṃ bheje) sṛjyamānaḥ) punaḥ punaḥ ‖
Manu01-29a/ hiṃsra-ahiṃsre mṛdu-krūre dharma-adharmāv ṛta-an-ṛte |
Manu01-29c/ yad yasya so 'adadhāt) sarge tat tasya svayam āvi;sat) ‖ 
Manu01-30a/ yathā-ṛtu-li:ngāny ṛtavaḥ svayam eva-ṛtuparyaye |
Manu01-30c/ svāni svāny abhipadyante) tathā karmāṇi dehinaḥ ‖
Manu01-31a/ lokānāṃ tu vivṛddhi-arthaṃ mukha-bāhu-ūru-pādataḥ |
Manu01-31c/ brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyaṃ vai;syaṃ ;sūdraṃ ca niravartayat) ‖

Manu01-32a/ dvidhā kṛtvā)-ātmano deham ardhena puruṣo 'abhavat) |
Manu01-32c/ ardhena nārī tasyāṃ sa virājam asṛjat) prabhuḥ ‖
Manu01-33a/ tapas taptvā)-asṛjad) yaṃ tu sa svayaṃ puruṣo virāṭ |
Manu01-33c/ taṃ māṃ vitta)-asya sarvasya sraṣṭāraṃ dvijasattamāḥ ‖
Manu01-34a/ ahaṃ prajāḥ sisṛkṣus tu tapas taptvā) su-du;scaram |
Manu01-34c/ patīn prajānām asṛjaṃ) maharṣīn ādito da;sa |
Manu01-35a/ marīcim atri-a:ngirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratum |
Manu01-35c/ pracetasaṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ ca bhṛguṃ nāradam eva ca ‖
Manu01-36a/ ete manūṃs tu saptān yān asṛjan) bhūritejasaḥ |
Manu01-36c/ devān devanikāyāṃ;s ca maharṣīṃ;s ca-amita-ojasaḥ ‖
Manu01-37a/ yakṣa-rakṣas-pi;sācāṃ;s ca gandharva-apsaraso 'asurān |
Manu01-37c/ nāgān sarpān suparṇāṃ;s ca pitṝṇāṃ;s ca pṛthaggaṇam ‖ [ṃ- pitṝṇāṃ]
Manu01-38a/ vidyuto 'a;sani-meghāṃ;s ca rohita-indradhanūṃṣi ca |
Manu01-38c/ ulkā-nirghāta-ketūṃ;s ca jyotīṃṣy uccāvacāni ca ‖
Manu01-39a/ kinnarān vānarān matsyān vividhāṃ;s ca viha:ngamān |
Manu01-39c/ pa;sūn mṛgān manuṣyāṃ;s ca vyālāṃ;s ca-ubhayatodataḥ ‖
Manu01-40a/ kṛmi-kīṭa-pata:ngāṃ;s ca yūkā-makṣika-matkuṇam |
Manu01-40c/ sarvaṃ ca daṃ;sa-ma;sakaṃ sthāvaraṃ ca pṛthagvidham ‖
Manu01-41a/ evam etair idaṃ sarvaṃ mad-niyogān mahātmabhiḥ |
Manu01-41c/ yathākarma tapoyogāt sṛṣṭaṃ) sthāvara-ja:ngamam ‖
Manu01-42a/ yeṣāṃ tu yādṛṣaṃ karma bhūtānām iha kīrtitam) |
Manu01-42c/ tat tathā vo 'abhidhāsyāmi) kramayogaṃ ca janmani ‖
Manu01-43a/ pa;sava;s ca mṛgā;s ca-eva vyālā;s ca-ubhayatodataḥ |
Manu01-43c/ rakṣāṃsi ca pi;sācā;s ca manuṣyā;s ca jarāyujāḥ ‖ [ṃ-manuṣā;s ca ]
Manu01-44a/ aṇḍājāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ sarpā nakrā matsyā;s ca kacchapāḥ |
Manu01-44c/ yāni ca-evaṃ-prakārāṇi sthalajāny audakāni ca ‖
Manu01-45a/ svedajaṃ daṃ;sa-ma;sakaṃ yūkā-makṣika-matkuṇam |
Manu01-45c/ ūṣmaṇa;s ca-upajāyante) yac ca-anyat kiṃ cid īdṛṣam ‖ 
Manu01-46a/ udbhijjāḥ sthāvarāḥ sarve bīja-kāṇḍaprarohiṇaḥ |
Manu01-46c/ oṣadhyaḥ phalapākāntā bahu-puṣpa-phala-upagāḥ ‖ 
Manu01-47a/ apuṣpāḥ phalavanto ye te vanaspatayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
Manu01-47c/ puṣpiṇaḥ phalina;s ca-eva vṛkṣās tu-ubhayataḥ smṛtāḥ ‖
Manu01-48a/ guccha-gulmaṃ tu vividhaṃ tathā-eva tṛṇajātayaḥ |
Manu01-48c/ bīja-kāṇḍaruhāṇy eva pratānā vallya eva ca ‖
Manu01-49a/ tamasā bahu-rūpeṇa veṣṭitāḥ) karmahetunā |
Manu01-49c/ antaḥ-saṃj;nā bhavanty) ete sukha-duḥkha-samanvitāḥ ‖
Manu01-50a/ etad-antās tu gatayo brahmādyāḥ samudāhṛtāḥ) |
Manu01-50c/ ghore 'asmin bhūtasaṃsāre nityaṃ satatayāyini ‖
Manu01-51a/ evaṃ sarvaṃ sa sṛṣṭvā)-idaṃ māṃ ca-acintya-parākramaḥ |
Manu01-51c/ ātmany antardadhe) bhūyaḥ kālaṃ kālena pīḍayan) ‖

Manu01-52a/ yadā sa devo jāgarti) tad evaṃ ceṣṭate) jagat |
Manu01-52c/ yadā svapiti) ;sānta-ātmā tadā sarvaṃ nimīlati) ‖
Manu01-53a/ tasmin svapiti tu svasthe karma-ātmānaḥ ;sarīriṇaḥ | [ṃ-svapati ]
Manu01-53c/ svakarmabhyo nivartante) mana;s ca glānim ṛcchati) ‖
Manu01-54a/ yugapat tu pralīyante) yadā tasmin mahātmani |
Manu01-54c/ tadā-ayaṃ sarvabhūtātmā sukhaṃ svapiti) nirvṛtaḥ ‖
Manu01-55a/ tamo 'ayaṃ tu samā;sritya) ciraṃ tiṣṭhati sa-indriyaḥ |
Manu01-55c/ na ca svaṃ kurute) karma tadā-utkrāmati) mūrtitaḥ ‖
Manu01-56a/ yadā-aṇumātriko bhūtvā) bījaṃ sthāsnu cariṣṇu ca |
Manu01-56c/ samāvi;sati) saṃsṛṣṭas) tadā mūrtiṃ vimu;ncati) ‖
Manu01-57a/ evaṃ sa jāgrat-svapnābhyām idaṃ sarvaṃ cara-acaram |
Manu01-57c/ saṃjīvayati) ca-ajasraṃ pramāpayati) ca-avyayaḥ ‖ 
Manu01-58a/ idaṃ ;sāstraṃ tu kṛtvā)-asau mām eva svayam āditaḥ |
Manu01-58c/ vidhivad grāhayām āsa) marīci-ādīṃs tv ahaṃ munīn ‖
Manu01-59a/ etad vo 'ayaṃ bhṛguḥ ;sāstraṃ ;srāvayiṣyaty) a;sesataḥ |
Manu01-59c/ etadd hi matto 'adhijage) sarvam eṣo 'akhilaṃ muniḥ ‖
Manu01-60a/ tatas tathā sa tena-ukto) maharṣi-manunā bhṛguḥ |
Manu01-60c/ tān abravīd) ṛṣīn sarvān prītātmā ;srūyatām) iti ‖

Manu01-61a/ svāyaṃbhuvasya-asya manoḥ ṣaḍvaṃ;syā manavo 'apare |
Manu01-61c/ sṛṣṭavantaḥ) prajāḥ svāḥ svā mahātmāno mahā-ojasaḥ ‖
Manu01-62a/ svārociṣa;s ca-uttama;s ca tāmaso raivatas tathā |
Manu01-62c/ cākṣuṣa;s ca mahātejā vivasvat-suta eva ca ‖
Manu01-63a/ svāyaṃbhuva-ādyāḥ sapta-ete manavo bhūritejasaḥ |
Manu01-63c/ sve sve 'antare sarvam idam utpādya)-āpu;s) cara-acaram ‖
Manu01-64a/ nimeṣā da;sa ca-aṣṭau ca kāṣṭhā triṃ;sat tu tāḥ kalā |
Manu01-64c/ triṃ;sat kalā muhūrtaḥ syād) ahorātraṃ tu tāvataḥ ‖
Manu01-65a/ ahorātre vibhajate) sūryo mānuṣa-daivike |
Manu01-65c/ rātriḥ svapnāya bhūtānāṃ ceṣṭāyai karmaṇām ahaḥ ‖
Manu01-66a/ pitrye rātri-ahanī māsaḥ pravibhāgas tu pakṣayoḥ | 
Manu01-66c/ karma-ceṣṭāsv ahaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ ;suklaḥ svapnāya ;sarvarī ‖
Manu01-67a/ daive rātri-ahanī varṣaṃ pravibhāgas tayoḥ punaḥ |
Manu01-67c/ ahas tatra-udagayanaṃ rātriḥ syād) dakṣiṇāyanam ‖
Manu01-68a/ brāhmasya tu kṣapāhasya yat pramāṇaṃ samāsataḥ |
Manu01-68c/ ekaika;so yugānāṃ tu krama;sas tan nibodhata) ‖
Manu01-69a/ catvāry āhuḥ) sahasrāṇi varsāṇāṃ tat kṛtaṃ yugam |
Manu01-69c/ tasya tāvat-;satī saṃdhyā saṃdhyāṃ;sa;s ca tathāvidhaḥ ‖
Manu01-70a/ itareṣu sa-saṃdhyeṣu sa-saṃdhyāṃ;seṣu ca triṣu |
Manu01-70c/ ekāpāyena vartante) sahasrāṇi ;satāni ca  ‖
Manu01-71a/ yad etat parisaṃkhyātam ādāv eva caturyugam | 
Manu01-71c/ etad dvāda;sasāhasraṃ devānāṃ yugam ucyate) ‖

Manu01-72a/ daivikānāṃ yugānāṃ tu sahasraṃ parisaṃkhyayā | 
Manu01-72c/ brāhmam ekam ahar j;neyaṃ) tāvatīṃ rātrim eva ca ‖ [ṃ-tāvatī rātrir eva ca ]
Manu01-73a/ tad vai yugasahasrāntaṃ brāhmaṃ puṇyam ahar viduḥ) |
Manu01-73c/ rātriṃ ca tāvatīm eva te 'ahorātravido janāḥ ‖
Manu01-74a/ tasya so 'ahar-ni;sasya-ante prasuptaḥ pratibudhyate) |
Manu01-74c/ pratibuddha;s ca sṛjati) manaḥ sad-asad-ātmakam ‖
Manu01-75a/ manaḥ sṛṣṭiṃ vikurute) codyamānaṃ sisṛkṣayā |
Manu01-75c/ ākā;saṃ jāyate) tasmāt tasya ;sabdaṃ guṇaṃ viduḥ) ‖
Manu01-76a/ ākā;sāt tu vikurvāṇāt) sarvagandhavahaḥ ;suciḥ |
Manu01-76c/ balavā;n jāyate) vāyuḥ sa vai spar;sa-guṇo mataḥ ‖
Manu01-77a/ vāyor api vikurvāṇād) virociṣṇu tamonudam |
Manu01-77c/ jyotir utpadyate) bhāsvat tad rūpa-guṇam ucyate) ‖
Manu01-78a/ jyotiṣa;s ca vikurvāṇād) āpo rasa-guṇāḥ smṛtāḥ) |
Manu01-78c/ adbhyo gandha-guṇā bhūmir ity eṣā sṛṣṭir āditaḥ ‖
Manu01-79a/ yad prāg dvāda;sasāhasram uditaṃ) daivikaṃ yugam |
Manu01-79c/ tad ekasaptati-guṇaṃ manvantaram iha-ucyate) ‖
Manu01-80a/ manvantarāṇy asaṃkhyāni sargaḥ saṃhāra eva ca | 
Manu01-80c/ krīḍann) iva-etat kurute) parameṣṭhī punaḥ punaḥ ‖

Manu01-81a/ catuṣpāt sakalo dharmaḥ satyaṃ ca-eva kṛte yuge |
Manu01-81c/ na-adharmeṇa-āgamaḥ ka;s cin manuṣyān prati vartate) ‖ [ṃ- upavartate ]
Manu01-82a/ itareṣv āgamād dharmaḥ pāda;sas tv avaropitaḥ) |
Manu01-82c/ caurika-anṛta-māyābhir dharma;s ca-apaiti) pāda;saḥ ‖
Manu01-83a/ a-rogāḥ sarvasiddhārthā;s caturvarṣa;sata-āyuṣaḥ |
Manu01-83c/ kṛte tretādiṣu hy eṣāṃ āyur hrasati) pāda;saḥ ‖ [V- vayo hrasati ]
Manu01-84a/ veda-uktam āyur martyānām ā;siṣa;s ca-eva karmaṇām |
Manu01-84c/ phalanty) anuyugaṃ loke prabhāva;s ca ;sarīriṇām ‖
Manu01-85a/ anye kṛtayuge dharmās tretāyāṃ dvāpare 'apare | [ṃ-pare ]
Manu01-85c/ anye kaliyuge nṝṇāṃ yugahrāsānurūpataḥ ‖
Manu01-86a/ tapaḥ paraṃ kṛtayuge tretāyāṃ j;nānam ucyate) |
Manu01-86c/ dvāpare yaj;nam evāhur) dānam ekaṃ kalau yuge ‖
Manu01-87a/ sarvasya-asya tu sargasya gupti-arthaṃ sa mahā-dyutiḥ |
Manu01-87c/ mukha-bāhu-ūru-pajjānāṃ pṛthakkarmāṇy akalpayat) ‖
Manu01-88a/ adhyāpanam adhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ yājanaṃ tathā |
Manu01-88c/ dānaṃ pratigrahaṃ ca-eva brāhmaṇānām akalpayat) ‖
Manu01-89a/ prajānāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ dānam ijyā-adhyayanam eva ca |
Manu01-89c/ viṣayeṣv a-prasakti;s ca kṣatriyasya samāsataḥ ) ‖ [ṃ-samādi;sat]
Manu01-90a/ pa;sūnāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ dānam ijyā-adhyayanam eva ca |
Manu01-90c/ vaṇikpathaṃ kusīdaṃ ca vai;syasya kṛṣim eva ca ‖
Manu01-91a/ ekam eva tu ;sūdrasya prabhuḥ karma samādi;sat) |
Manu01-91c/ eteṣām eva varṇānāṃ ;su;srūṣām anasūyayā ‖

Manu01-92a/ ūrdhvaṃ nābher medhyataraḥ puruṣaḥ parikīrtitaḥ) |
Manu01-92c/ tasmān medhyatamaṃ tv asya mukham uktaṃ) svayaṃbhuvā ‖
Manu01-93a/ uttamā:nga-udbhavāj jyeṣṭhyād brahmaṇa;s ca-eva dhāraṇāt | [ṃ-jyaiṣṭhyād]
Manu01-93c/ sarvasya-eva-asya sargasya dharmato brāhmaṇaḥ prabhuḥ ‖
Manu01-94a/ taṃ hi svayaṃbhūḥ svād āsyāt tapas taptvā)-ādito 'asṛjat) |
Manu01-94c/ havya-kavyābhivāhyāya sarvasya-asya ca guptaye ‖

<B 4> 	

Manu04-01a/ caturtham āyuṣo bhāgam uṣitvā)-ādyaṃ gurau dvijāḥ  |

Manu04-07a/ kusūla-dhānyako vā syāt) kumbhī-dhānyaka eva vā |
Manu04-07c/ tryaha-ehiko vā-api bhaved) a-;svastanika eva vā ‖
Manu04-08a/ caturṇām api ca-eteṣāṃ dvijānāṃ gṛhamedhinām |

Manu05-01a/ ;srutvā-etān ṛṣayo dharmān snātakasya yathā-uditān |

Manu05-03c/ ;srūyatāṃ yena doṣeṇa mṛtyur viprān jighāṃsati ‖

Manu05-32a/ krītvā svayaṃ vā-apy utpādya para-upakṛtam eva vā |
Manu05-32c/ devān pitṝṃ;s ca-arcayitvā khādan māṃsaṃ na duṣyati ‖

Manu05-33c/ jagdhvā hy a-vidhinā māṃsaṃ pretas tair adyate 'a-va;saḥ ‖
Manu05-34a/ na tādṛ;saṃ bhavaty eno mṛgahantur dhanārthinaḥ |
Manu05-34c/ yādṛ;saṃ bhavati pretya vṛthāmāṃsāni khādataḥ ‖
Manu05-35a/ niyuktas tu yathānyāyaṃ yo māṃsaṃ na-atti mānavaḥ |
Manu05-35c/ sa pretya pa;sutāṃ yāti saṃbhavān ekaviṃ;satim ‖ 
Manu05-36a/ asaṃskṛtān pa;sūn mantrair na-adyād vipraḥ kadā cana |
Manu05-36c/ mantrais tu saṃskṛtān adyāt-;sā;svataṃ vidhim āsthitaḥ ‖
Manu05-37a/ kuryād ghṛtapa;suṃ sa:nge kuryāt piṣṭapa;suṃ tathā |
Manu05-37c/ na tv eva tu vṛthā hantuṃ pa;sum icchet kadā cana ‖
Manu05-38a/ yāvanti pa;suromāṇi tāvatkṛtvo ha māraṇam |
Manu05-38c/ vṛthāpa;sughnaḥ prāpnoti pretya janmani janmani ‖
Manu05-39a/ yaj;nārthaṃ pa;savaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayam eva svayaṃbhuvā | 
Manu05-39c/ yaj;no 'asya bhūtyai sarvasya tasmād yaj;ne vadho 'a-vadhaḥ ‖
Manu05-40a/ oṣadhyaḥ pa;savo vṛkṣās tirya;ncaḥ pakṣiṇas tathā |
Manu05-40c/ yaj;nārthaṃ nidhanaṃ prāptāḥ prāpnuvanty utsṛtīḥ  punaḥ ‖ [ṃ-ucchritīḥ]
Manu05-41a/ madhuparke ca yaj;ne ca pitṛ-daivatakarmaṇi |
Manu05-41c/ atra-eva pa;savo hiṃsyā na-anyatra-ity abravīn manuḥ ‖
Manu05-42a/ eṣv artheṣu pa;sūn hiṃsan vedatattvārthavid dvijaḥ |
Manu05-42c/ ātmānaṃ ca pa;suṃ ca-eva gamayaty uttamaṃ gatim ‖
Manu05-43a/ gṛhe gurāv araṇye vā nivasann ātmavān dvijaḥ |
Manu05-43c/ na-a-vedavihitāṃ hiṃsām āpady api samācaret ‖
Manu05-44a/ yā vedavihitā hiṃsā niyatā-asmiṃ;s cara-acare |
Manu05-44c/ ahiṃsām eva tāṃ vidyād vedād dharmo hi nirbabhau ‖
Manu05-45a/ yo 'ahiṃsakāni bhūtāni hinasty ātmasukha-icchayā |
Manu05-45c/ sa jīvāṃ;s ca mṛta;s ca-eva na kva cit sukham edhate ‖
Manu05-46a/ yo bandhanavadhakle;sān prāṇināṃ na cikīrṣati |
Manu05-46c/ sa sarvasya hitaprepsuḥ sukham atyantam a;snute ‖
Manu05-47a/ yad dhyāyati yat kurute ratiṃ badhnāti yatra ca |
Manu05-47c/ tad avāpnoty ayatnena yo hinasti na kiṃ cana ‖ 
Manu05-48a/ na-a-kṛtvā prāṇināṃ hiṃsāṃ māṃsam utpadyate kva cit |
Manu05-48c/ na ca prāṇivadhaḥ svargyas tasmān māṃsaṃ vivarjayet ‖
Manu05-49a/ samutpattiṃ ca māṃsasya vadha-bandhau ca dehinām |
Manu05-49c/ prasamīkṣya nivarteta sarvamāṃsasya bhakṣaṇāt ‖
Manu05-50a/ na bhakṣayati yo māṃsaṃ vidhiṃ hitvā pi;sācavat |
Manu05-50c/ na loke priyatāṃ yāti vyādhibhi;s ca na pīḍyate ‖
Manu05-51a/ anumantā vi;sasitā nihantā kraya-vikrayī |
Manu05-51c/ saṃskartā ca-upahartā ca khādaka;s ca-iti ghātakāḥ ‖
Manu05-52a/ svamāṃsaṃ paramāṃsena yo vardhayitum icchati |
Manu05-52c/ an-abhyarcya pitṝn devāṃs tato 'anyo na-asty apuṇyakṛt ‖
Manu05-53a/ varṣe varṣe 'a;svamedhena yo yajeta ;sataṃ samāḥ |
Manu05-53c/ māṃsāni ca na khāded yas tayoḥ puṇyaphalaṃ samam ‖
Manu05-54a/ phala-mūla-a;sanair medhyair muni-annānāṃ ca bhojanaiḥ |
Manu05-54c/ na tat phalam avāpnoti yat-māṃsaparivarjanāt ‖
Manu05-55a/ māṃ sa bhakṣayitā-amutra yasya māṃsam iha-admy aham |
Manu05-55c/ etat-māṃsasya māṃsatvaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ ‖
Manu05-56a/ na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣo na madye na ca maithune |
Manu05-56c/ pravṛttir eṣā bhūtānāṃ nivṛttis tu mahāphalā ‖

Manu05-57a/ preta;suddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi) dravya;suddhiṃ tathā-eva ca |
Manu05-57c/ caturṇām api varṇānāṃ yathāvad anupūrva;saḥ ‖
Manu05-58a/ dantajāte 'anujāte ca kṛta-cūḍe ca saṃsthite |
Manu05-58c/ a;suddhā bāndhavāḥ sarve sūtake ca tathā-ucyate ‖
Manu05-59a/ da;sāhaṃ ;sāvam ā;saucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate |
Manu05-59c/ arvāk saṃcayanād asthnāṃ tryaham ekāham eva vā ‖
Manu05-60a/ sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate |
Manu05-60c/ samānodakabhāvas tu janma-nāmnor a-vedane ‖
Manu05-61a/ yathā-idaṃ ;sāvam ā;saucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate / [not in ṃ]
Manu05-61c/ janane 'apy evam eva syāt-nipuṇaṃ ;suddhim icchatām // [not in ṃ]
Manu05-62a[61ṃa]/ sarveṣāṃ ;sāvam ā;saucaṃ mātā-pitros tu sūtakam / [ṃ: janane 'apy evam eva syān mātā-pitros tu sūtakam /]
Manu05-62c[61ṃc]/ sūtakaṃ mātur eva syād) upaspṛ;sya pitā ;suciḥ //
Manu05-63a[62ṃa]/nirasya tu pumān-;sukram upaspṛsya-eva ;sudhyati |
Manu05-63c[62ṃc]/ baijikād abhisaṃbandhād anurundhyād aghaṃ tryaham ‖ 
Manu05-64a[63ṃa]/ahnā ca-ekena rātryā ca trirātrair eva ca tribhiḥ |
Manu05-64c[63ṃc]/ ;sava-spṛ;so vi;sudhyanti tryahād udakadāyinaḥ ‖
Manu05-65a[64ṃa]/guroḥ pretasya ;siṣyas tu pitṛmedhaṃ samācaran |
Manu05-65c[64ṃc]/ pretahāraiḥ samaṃ tatra da;sarātreṇa ;sudhyati ‖ [ṃ-pretāhāraiḥ ]
Manu05-66a[65ṃa]/rātribhir māsa-tulyābhir garbhasrāve vi;sudhyati |
Manu05-66c[65ṃc]/ rajasy uparate sādhvī snānena strī rajasvalā ‖
Manu05-67a[66ṃa]/nṛṇām a-kṛtacūḍānāṃ vi;suddhir nai;sikī smṛtā |
Manu05-67c[66ṃc]/ nirvṛtta-cūḍakānāṃ tu trirātrāt-;suddhir iṣyate ‖ [ṃ- nirvṛtta-muṇḍakānāṃ ]
Manu05-68a[67ṃa]/ūna-dvivārṣikaṃ pretaṃ nidadhyur bāndhavā bahiḥ |
Manu05-68c[67ṃc]/ alaṃkṛtya ;sucau bhūmāv asthisaṃcayanād ṛte ‖
Manu05-69a[68ṃa]/na-asya kāryo 'agnisaṃskāro na ca kāryā-udakakriyā |
Manu05-69c[68ṃc]/ araṇye kāṣṭhavat tyaktvā kṣapeyus tryaham eva tu  ‖ [ṃ- kṣapeta tryaham eva ca]
Manu05-70a[69ṃa]/na-a-trivarṣasya kartavyā bāndhavair udakakriyā |
Manu05-70c[69ṃc]/ jāta-dantasya vā kuryur nāmni vā-api kṛte sati ‖
Manu05-71a[70ṃa]/sa-brahmacāriṇy ekāham atīte kṣapaṇam smṛtam |
Manu05-71c[70ṃc]/ janmany eka-udakānāṃ tu trirātrāt-;suddhir iṣyate ‖
Manu05-72a[71ṃa]/strīṇām a-saṃskṛtānāṃ tu tryahāt-;sudhyanti bāndhavāḥ |
Manu05-72c[71ṃc]/ yathā-uktena-eva kalpena ;sudhyanti tu sa-nābhayaḥ ‖
Manu05-73a[72ṃa]/a-kṣāra-lavaṇa-annāḥ syur nimajjeyu;s ca te tryaham |
Manu05-73c[72ṃc]/ māṃsā;sanaṃ ca na-a;snīyuḥ ;sayīraṃ;s ca pṛthak kṣitau ‖
Manu05-74a[73ṃa]/saṃnidhāv eṣa vai kalpaḥ ;sāva-ā;saucasya kīrtitaḥ |

Manu05-78a[77ṃa]/ bāle de;sāntarasthe ca pṛthak-piṇḍe ca saṃsthite |

Manu05-81a[80ṃa]/ ;srotriye tu-upasaṃpanne trirātram a;sucir bhavet |

[Manu05-123Ka/ madyair mūtraiḥ purīṣair vā ṣṭhīvanaih pūya;soṇitaiḥ / [not in ṃ] 
[Manu05-123Kb/ saṃspṛṣṭaṃ na-eva ;suddhyeta punaḥpākena mṛt-mayam // not in ṃ]

Manu05-124c[122ṃc]/ gavāṃ ca parivāsena bhūmiḥ ;sudhyati pa;ncabhiḥ ‖ [ṃ-;suddhyati]
Manu05-125a[123ṃa]/ pakṣi-jagdhaṃ gavā ghrātam avadhūtam avakṣutam |
Manu05-125c[123ṃc]/ dūṣitaṃ ke;sa-kīṭai;s ca mṛt-prakṣepeṇa ;sudhyati ‖
Manu05-126a[124ṃa]/ yāvat-na-apaity a-medhyāktād gandho lepa;s ca tat-kṛtaḥ |
Manu05-126c[124ṃc]/ tāvan mṛd-vāri ca-ādeyaṃ sarvāsu dravya;suddhiṣu ‖
Manu05-127a[125ṃa]/ trīṇi devāḥ pavitrāṇi brāhmaṇānām akalpayan |
Manu05-127c[125ṃc]/ a-dṛṣṭam adbhir nirṇiktaṃ yac ca vācā pra;sasyate ‖
Manu05-128a[126ṃa]/ āpaḥ ;suddhā bhūmigatā vaitṛṣṇyaṃ yāsu gor bhavet |
Manu05-128c[126ṃc]/ a-vyāptā;s ced a-medhyena gandha-varṇa-rasa-anvitāḥ ‖
Manu05-129a[127ṃa]/ nityaṃ ;suddhaḥ kāru-hastaḥ paṇye yac ca prasāritam | [ṃ-paṇyaṃ]
Manu05-129c[127ṃc]/ brahmacārigataṃ bhaikṣyaṃ nityaṃ medhyam iti sthitiḥ ‖
Manu05-130a[128ṃa]/ nityam āsyaṃ ;suci strīṇāṃ ;sakuniḥ phalapātane |
Manu05-130c[128ṃc]/ prasrave ca ;sucir vatsaḥ ;svā mṛgagrahaṇe ;suciḥ ‖
Manu05-131a[129ṃa]/ ;svabhir hatasya yan māṃsaṃ ;suci tan manur abravīt |
Manu05-131c[129ṃc]/ kravyādbhi;s ca hatasya-anyai;s caṇḍālādyai;s ca dasyubhiḥ ‖
Manu05-132a[130ṃa]/ ūrdhvaṃ nābher yāni khāni tāni medhyāni sarva;saḥ |
Manu05-132c[130ṃc]/ yāny adhas tāny a-medhyāni dehāc ca-eva malā;s cyutāḥ ‖
Manu05-133a[131ṃa]/ makṣikā vipruṣa;s chāyā gaur a;svaḥ sūryara;smayaḥ |
Manu05-133c[131ṃc]/ rajo bhūr vāyur agni;s ca spar;se medhyāni nirdi;set ‖

Manu05-134a[132ṃa]/ viṣ-mūtra-utsarga-;suddhi-arthaṃ mṛt-vāry ādeyam arthavat |
Manu05-134c[132ṃc]/ daihikānāṃ malānāṃ ca ;suddhiṣu dvāda;sasv api ‖
Manu05-135a[133ṃa]/ vasā ;sukram asṛj-majjā mūtra-viṣ-ghrāṇa-karṇa-viṣ- |
Manu05-135c[133ṃc]/ ;sle;sma a;sru dūṣikā svedo dvāda;sa-ete nṛṇāṃ malāḥ ‖
Manu05-136a[134ṃa]/ ekā li:nge gude tisras tathā-ekatra kare da;sa |
Manu05-136c[134ṃc]/ ubhayoḥ sapta dātavyā mṛdaḥ ;suddhim abhīpsatā ‖
Manu05-137a[135ṃa]/ etat-;saucaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām |
Manu05-137c[135ṃc]/ triguṇaṃ syād vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ tu caturguṇam ‖
Manu05-138a[136ṃa]/ kṛtvā mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā khāny ācānta upaspṛ;set |
Manu05-138c[136ṃc]/ vedam adhyeṣyamāṇa;s ca annam a;snaṃ;s ca sarvadā ‖
Manu05-139a[137ṃa]/ trir ācāmed apaḥ pūrvaṃ dviḥ pramṛjyāt tato mukham |
Manu05-139c[137ṃc]/ ;sarīraṃ ;saucam icchan hi strī ;sūdras tu sakṛt sakṛt ‖
Manu05-140a[138ṃa]/ ;sūdrāṇāṃ māsikaṃ kāryaṃ vapanaṃ nyāyavartinām |
Manu05-140c[138ṃc]/ vai;syavat-;saucakalpa;s ca dvija-ucchiṣṭaṃ ca bhojanam ‖
Manu05-141a[139ṃa]/ na-ucchiṣṭaṃ kurvate mukhyā vipruṣo 'a:ngaṃ na yānti yāḥ |
Manu05-141c[139ṃc]/ na ;sma;srūṇi gatāny āsyaṃ na dantāntar-adhiṣṭhitam ‖
Manu05-142a[140ṃa]/ spṛ;santi bindavaḥ pādau ya ācāmayataḥ parān |
Manu05-142c[140ṃc]/ bhaumikais te samā j;neyā na tair āprayato bhavet ‖ [ṃ- a-prayato]
Manu05-143a[141ṃa]/ ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭo dravya-hastaḥ kathaṃ cana |
Manu05-143c[141ṃc]/ a-nidhāya-eva tad dravyam ācāntaḥ ;sucitām iyāt ‖
Manu05-144a[142ṃa]/ vānto viriktaḥ snātvā tu ghṛtaprā;sanam ācaret |
Manu05-144c[142ṃc]/ ācāmed eva bhuktvā-annaṃ snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam ‖
Manu05-145a[143ṃa]/ suptvā kṣutvā ca bhuktvā ca niṣṭhīvya-uktvā-anṛtāni ca |
Manu05-145c[143ṃc]/ pītvā-apo 'adhyeṣyamāṇa;s ca ācāmet prayato 'api san ‖
Manu05-146a[144ṃa]/ eṣāṃ ;saucavidhiḥ kṛtsno dravya;suddhis tathā-eva ca | [ṃ-eṣa]
Manu05-146c[144ṃc]/ ukto vaḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ strīṇāṃ dharmān nibodhata ‖
Manu05-147a[145ṃa]/ bālayā vā yuvatyā vā vṛddhayā vā-api yoṣitā |
Manu05-147c[145ṃc]/ na svātantryeṇa kartavyaṃ kiṃ cid kāryaṃ gṛheṣv api ‖
Manu05-148a[146ṃa]/ bālye pitur va;se tiṣṭhet pāṇigrāhasya yauvane |
Manu05-148c[146ṃc]/ putrāṇāṃ bhartari prete na bhajet strī svatantratām ‖
Manu05-149a[147ṃa]/ pitrā bhartrā sutair vā-api na-icched viraham ātmanaḥ |
Manu05-149c[147ṃc]/ eṣāṃ hi viraheṇa strī garhye kuryād ubhe kule ‖
Manu05-150a[148ṃa]/ sadā prahṛṣṭayā bhāvyaṃ gṛhakārye ca dakṣayā |
Manu05-150c[148ṃc]/ susaṃskṛta-upaskarayā vyaye ca-amukta-hastayā ‖
Manu05-151a[149ṃa]/ yasmai dadyāt pitā tv enāṃ bhrātā vā-anumate pituḥ |
Manu05-151c[149ṃc]/ taṃ ;su;srūṣeta jīvantaṃ saṃsthitaṃ ca na la:nghayet ‖
Manu05-152a[150ṃa]/ ma:ngalārthaṃ svastyayanaṃ yaj;na;s ca-āsāṃ prajāpateḥ |
Manu05-152c[150ṃc]/ prayujyate vivāhe tu pradānaṃ svāmya-kāraṇam ‖
Manu05-153a[151ṃa]/ an-ṛtāv ṛtukāle ca mantra-saṃskārakṛt patiḥ |
Manu05-153c[151ṃc]/ sukhasya nityaṃ dātā-iha paraloke ca yoṣitaḥ ‖
Manu05-154a[152ṃa]/ vi-;sīlaḥ kāma-vṛtto vā guṇair vā parivarjitaḥ |
Manu05-154c[152ṃc]/ upacāryaḥ striyā sādhvyā satataṃ devavat patiḥ ‖
Manu05-155a[153ṃa]/ na-asti strīṇāṃ pṛthag yaj;no na vrataṃ na-apy upoṣaṇam | [ṃ-upoṣitam]
Manu05-155c[153ṃc]/ patiṃ ;su;srūṣate yena tena svarge mahīyate ‖
Manu05-156a[154ṃa]/ pāṇigrāhasya sādhvī strī jīvato vā mṛtasya vā |
Manu05-156c[154ṃc]/ patilokam abhīpsantī na-ācaret kiṃ cid a-priyam ‖
Manu05-157a[155ṃa]/ kāmaṃ tu ksapayed dehaṃ puṣpa-mūla-phalaiḥ ;subhaiḥ |
Manu05-157c[155ṃc]/ na tu nāma-api gṛhṇīyāt patyau prete parasya tu ‖
Manu05-158a[156ṃa]/ āsīta-ā maraṇāt ksāntā niyatā brahmacāriṇī |
Manu05-158c[156ṃc]/ yo dharma ekapatnīnāṃ kā:nkṣantī tam anuttamam ‖
Manu05-159a[157ṃa]/ anekāni sahasrāṇi kumāra-brahmacāriṇām |
Manu05-159c[157ṃc]/ divaṃ gatāni viprāṇām a-kṛtvā kulasaṃtatim ‖
Manu05-160a[158ṃa]/ mṛte bhartari sāḍhvī strī brahmacarye vyavasthitā |
Manu05-160c[158ṃc]/ svargaṃ gacchaty a-putrā-api yathā te brahmacāriṇaḥ ‖
Manu05-161a[159ṃa]/ apatyalobhād yā tu strī bhartāram ativartate |
Manu05-161c[159ṃc]/ sā-iha nindām avāpnoti paralokāc ca hīyate ‖
Manu05-162a[160ṃa]/ na-anya-utpannā prajā-asti-iha na ca-apy anyaparigrahe | [ṃ-na ca-anyasya parigrahe]
Manu05-162c[160ṃc]/ na dvitīya;s ca sādhvīnāṃ kva cid bhartā-upadi;syate ‖
Manu05-163a[161ṃa]/ patiṃ hitvā-apakṛṣṭaṃ svam utkṛṣṭaṃ yā niṣevate | [ṃ-hitvā-avakṛṣṭaṃ]
Manu05-163c[161ṃc]/ nindyā-eva sā bhavel loke para-pūrvā-iti ca-ucyate ‖
Manu05-164a[162ṃa]/ vyabhicārāt tu bhartuḥ strī loke prāpnoti nindyatām | [ṃ-vyabhicāre tu]
Manu05-164c[162ṃc]/ ;sṛgāla-yoniṃ prāpnoti pāpa-rogai;s ca pīḍyate ‖
Manu05-165a[163ṃa]/ patiṃ yā na-abhicarati mano-vāg-dehasaṃyutā | [ṃ- -dehasaṃyatā]
Manu05-165c[163ṃc]/ sā bhartṛlokam āpnoti sadbhiḥ sādhvī-iti ca-ucyate ‖
Manu05-166a[164ṃa]/ anena nārī vṛttena mano-vāg-dehasaṃyatā |
Manu05-166c[164ṃc]/ iha-agryāṃ kīrtim āpnoti patilokaṃ paratra ca ‖
Manu05-167a[165ṃa]/ evaṃ vṛttāṃ sa-varṇāṃ strīṃ dvijātiḥ pūrvamāriṇīm |
Manu05-167c[165ṃc]/ dāhayed agnihotreṇa yaj;napātrai;s ca dharmavit ‖
Manu05-168a[166ṃa]/ bhāryāyai pūrvamāriṇyai dattvā-agnīn antyakarmaṇi |
Manu05-168c[166ṃc]/ punar dārakriyāṃ kuryāt punar ādhānam eva ca ‖
Manu05-169a[167ṃa]/ anena vidhinā nityaṃ pa;ncayaj;nān na hāpayet |
Manu05-169c[167ṃc]/ dvitīyam āyuṣo bhāgaṃ kṛta-dāro gṛhe vaset ‖

<B 6>

Manu06-01a/ evaṃ gṛhā;srame sthitvā vidhivat snātako dvijaḥ |
Manu06-01c/ vane vaset tu niyato yathāvad vijita-indriyaḥ ‖
Manu06-02a/ gṛhasthas tu yathā pa;syed valī-palitam ātmanaḥ |
Manu06-02c/ apatyasya-eva ca-apatyaṃ tadā-araṇyaṃ samā;srayet ‖
Manu06-03a/ saṃtyajya grāmyam āhāraṃ sarvaṃ ca-eva paricchadam |
Manu06-03c/ putreṣu bhāryāṃ nikṣipya vanaṃ gacchet saha-eva vā ‖
Manu06-04a/ agnihotraṃ samādāya gṛhyaṃ ca-agniparicchadam |
Manu06-04c/ grāmād araṇyaṃ niḥsṛtya nivasen niyata-indriyaḥ ‖ [ṃ-niṣkramya]
Manu06-05a/ muni-annair vividhair medhyaiḥ ;sāka-mūla-phalena vā |
Manu06-05c/ etān eva mahāyaj;nān nirvaped vidhipūrvakam ‖
Manu06-06a/ vasīta carma cīraṃ vā sāyaṃ snāyāt prage tathā |
Manu06-06c/ jaṭā;s ca bibhṛyān nityaṃ ;sma;sru-loma-nakhāni ca ‖
Manu06-07a/ yad-bhakṣyaṃ syād tato dadyād baliṃ bhikṣāṃ ca ;saktitaḥ | [ṃ-yadbhakṣaḥ ]
Manu06-07c/ ap-mūla-phala-bhikṣābhir arcayed ā;sramāgatān ‖ [ṃ:ā;sramāgatam ]
Manu06-08a/ svādhyāye nityayuktaḥ syād dānto maitraḥ samāhitaḥ |
Manu06-08c/ dātā nityam an-ādātā sarvabhūtānukampakaḥ ‖
Manu06-09a/ vaitānikaṃ ca juhuyād agnihotraṃ yathāvidhi |
Manu06-09c/ dar;sam a-skandayan parva paurṇamāsaṃ ca yogataḥ ‖
Manu06-10a/ ṛkṣeṣṭy-āgrayaṇaṃ ca-eva cāturmāsyāni ca-āharet | [ṃ-dar;seṣṭy-āgrayaṇaṃ]
Manu06-10c/ turāyaṇaṃ ca krama;so dakṣasyāyanam eva ca ‖ [Kṃ-dākṣasyāyanam ]
Manu06-11a/ vāsanta-;sāradair medhyair muni-annaiḥ svayam āhṛtaiḥ |
Manu06-11c/ puroḍā;sāṃ;s carūṃ;s ca-eva vidhivat-nirvapet pṛthak ‖
Manu06-12a/ devatābhyas tu tadd hutvā vanyaṃ medhyataraṃ haviḥ |
Manu06-12c/ ;seṣam ātmani yu;njīta lavaṇaṃ ca svayaṃ kṛtam ‖
Manu06-13a/ sthalaja-audaka;sākāni puṣpa-mūla-phalāni ca |
Manu06-13c/ medhyavṛkṣa-udbhavāny adyāt snehāṃ;s ca phala-saṃbhavān |
Manu06-14a/ varjayen madhu māṃsaṃ ca bhaumāni kavakāni ca |
Manu06-14c/ bhūstṛṇaṃ ;sigrukaṃ ca-eva ;sle;smātaka-phalāni ca ‖
Manu06-15a/ tyajed ā;svayuje māsi muni-annaṃ pūrvasaṃcitam |
Manu06-15c/ jīrṇāni ca-eva vāsāṃsi ;sāka-mūla-phalāni ca ‖
Manu06-16a/ na phālakṛṣṭam a;snīyād utsṛṣṭam api kena cit |
Manu06-16c/ na grāmajātāny ārto 'api mūlāṇi ca phalāni ca ‖ [ṃ-puṣpāni ca phalāni ca ]
Manu06-17a/ agnipakva-a;sano vā syāt kālapakvabhuj- eva vā |
Manu06-17c/ a;sma-kuṭṭo bhaved vā-api danta-ulūkhaliko 'api vā ‖
Manu06-18a/ sadyaḥ prakṣālako vā syān māsa-saṃcayiko 'api vā |
Manu06-18c/ ṣaṇmāsa-nicayo vā syāt samā-nicaya eva vā ‖
Manu06-19a/ naktaṃ ca-annaṃ sama;snīyād divā vā-āhṛtya ;saktitaḥ |
Manu06-19c/ caturthakāliko vā syāt syād vā-apy aṣṭama-kālikaḥ ‖
Manu06-20a/ cāndrāyaṇavidhānair vā ;sukla-kṛṣṇe ca vartayet |
Manu06-20c/ pakṣāntayor vā-apy a;snīyād yavāgūṃ kvathitāṃ sakṛt ‖
Manu06-21a/ puṣpa-mūla-phalair vā-api kevalair vartayet sadā |
Manu06-21c/ kālapakvaiḥ svayaṃ ;sīrṇair vaikhānasamate sthitaḥ ‖
Manu06-22a/ bhūmau viparivarteta tiṣṭhed vā prapadair dinam |
Manu06-22c/ sthāna-āsanābhyāṃ viharet savaneṣu-upayann apaḥ ‖
Manu06-23a/ grīṣme pa;nca-tapās tu syād varṣāsv abhra-avakā;sikaḥ |
Manu06-23c/ ārdra-vāsās tu hemante krama;so vardhayaṃs tapaḥ ‖
Manu06-24a/ upaspṛ;saṃs triṣavaṇaṃ pitṝn devāṃ;s ca tarpayet |
Manu06-24c/ tapas-caraṃ;s ca-ugrataraṃ ;soṣayed deham ātmanaḥ ‖
Manu06-25a/ agnīn ātmani vaitānān samāropya yathāvidhi |
Manu06-25c/ an-agnir a-niketaḥ syān munir mūla-phala-a;sanaḥ ‖
Manu06-26a/ aprayatnaḥ sukhārtheṣu brahmacārī dharā-ā;sayaḥ |
Manu06-26c/ ;saraṇeṣv a-mama;s ca-eva vṛkṣamūla-niketanaḥ ‖
Manu06-27a/ tāpaseṣv eva vipreṣu yātrikaṃ bhaikṣam āharet |
Manu06-27c/ gṛhamedhiṣu ca-anyeṣu dvijeṣu vanavāsiṣu ‖
Manu06-28a/ grāmād āhṛtya vā-a;snīyād aṣṭau grāsān vane vasan |
Manu06-28c/ pratigṛhya puṭena-eva pāṇinā ;sakalena vā ‖
Manu06-29a/ etā;s ca-anyā;s ca seveta dīkṣā vipro vane vasan |
Manu06-29c/ vividhā;s ca-aupaniṣadīr ātmasaṃsiddhaye ;srutīḥ ‖
Manu06-30a/ ṛṣibhir brāhmaṇai;s ca-eva gṛhasthair eva sevitāḥ |
Manu06-30c/ vidyā-tapo-vivṛddhyarthaṃ ;sarīrasya ca ;suddhaye ‖
Manu06-31a/ aparājitāṃ vā-āsthāya vrajed di;sam ajihmagaḥ |
Manu06-31c/ ā nipātāt-;sarīrasya yukto vāri-anila-a;sanaḥ ‖
Manu06-32a/ āsāṃ maharṣicaryāṇāṃ tyaktvā-anyatamayā tanum |
Manu06-32c/ vīta-;soka-bhayo vipro brahmaloke mahīyate ‖

Manu06-33a/ vaneṣu ca vihṛtya-evaṃ tṛtīyaṃ bhāgam āyuṣaḥ |
Manu06-33c/ caturtham āyuṣo bhāgaṃ tyakvā sa:ngān parivrajet ‖
Manu06-34a/ ā;sramād ā;sramaṃ gatvā huta-homo jita-indriyaḥ |
Manu06-34c/ bhikṣā-bali-pari;srāntaḥ pravrajan pretya vardhate ‖
Manu06-35a/ ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtya mano mokṣe nive;sayet |
Manu06-35c/ an-apākṛtya mokṣaṃ tu sevamāno vrajaty adhaḥ ‖
Manu06-36a/ adhītya vidhivad vedān putrāṃ;s ca-utpādya dharmataḥ |
Manu06-36c/ iṣṭvā ca ;saktito yaj;nair mano mokṣe nive;sayet ‖
Manu06-37a/ an-adhītya dvijo vedān an-utpādya tathā sutān | [ṃ-tathā prajām ]
Manu06-37c/ an-iṣṭvā ca-eva yaj;nai;s ca mokṣam icchan vrajaty adhaḥ ‖
Manu06-38a/ prājāpatyaṃ nirupya-iṣṭiṃ sarvavedasa-dakṣiṇām | [ṃ-sārvavedasadakṣiṇām]
Manu06-38c/ ātmany agnīn samāropya brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajed gṛhāt ‖
Manu06-39a/ yo dattvā sarvabhūtebhyaḥ pravrajaty abhayaṃ gṛhāt |
Manu06-39c/ tasya tejomayā lokā bhavanti brahmavādinaḥ ‖
Manu06-40a/ yasmād aṇu-api bhūtānāṃ dvijān na-utpadyate bhayam |
Manu06-40c/ tasya dehād vimuktasya bhayaṃ na-asti kuta;s cana ‖
Manu06-41a/ agārād abhiniṣkrāntaḥ pavitra-upacito muniḥ |
Manu06-41c/ samupoḍheṣu kāmeṣu nir-apekṣaḥ parivrajet ‖
Manu06-42a/ eka eva caren nityaṃ siddhyartham asahāyavān |
Manu06-42c/ siddhim ekasya saṃpa;syan na jahāti na hīyate ‖ [ṃ-siddham]
Manu06-43a/ an-agnir a-niketaḥ syād grāmam annārtham ā;srayet |
Manu06-43c/ upekṣako 'a-saṃkusuko munir bhāvasamāhitaḥ | [ṃ-a-sāṃkusuko ]
Manu06-44a/ kapālaṃ vṛkṣamūlāni kucelam asahāyatā | [ṃ-kucailam ]
Manu06-44c/ samatā ca-eva sarvasminn etat-muktasya lakṣaṇam ‖
Manu06-45a/ na-abhinandeta maraṇaṃ na-abhinandeta jīvitam |
Manu06-45c/ kālam eva pratīkṣeta nirve;saṃ bhṛtako yathā ‖
Manu06-46a/ dṛṣṭipūtaṃ nyaset pādaṃ vastrapūtaṃ jalaṃ pibet |
Manu06-46c/ satyapūtāṃ vaded vācaṃ manaḥpūtaṃ samācaret ‖
Manu06-47a/ ativādāṃs titikṣeta na-avamanyeta kaṃ cana |
Manu06-47c/ na ca-imaṃ deham ā;sritya vairaṃ kurvīta kena cit ‖
Manu06-48a/ kruddhyantaṃ na pratikrudhyed ākruṣṭaḥ ku;salaṃ vadet |
Manu06-48c/ saptadvārāvakīrṇāṃ ca na vācam anṛtāṃ vadet ‖
Manu06-49a/ adhyātma-ratir āsīno nir-apekṣo nir-āmiṣaḥ |
Manu06-49c/ ātmanā-eva sahāyena sukhārthī vicared iha ‖
Manu06-50a/ na ca-utpāta-nimittābhyāṃ na nakṣatra-a:ngavidyayā |
Manu06-50c/ na-anu;sāsana-vādābhyāṃ bhikṣāṃ lipseta karhi cit ‖
Manu06-51a/ na tāpasair brāhmaṇair vā vayobhir api vā ;svabhiḥ |
Manu06-51c/ ākīrṇaṃ bhikṣukair vā-anyair agāram upasaṃvrajet ‖
Manu06-52a/ kḷpta-ke;sa-nakha-;sma;sruḥ pātrī daṇḍī kusumbhavān |
Manu06-52c/ vicaren niyato nityaṃ sarvabhūtāny a-pīḍayan ‖
Manu06-53a/ ataijasāni pātrāṇi tasya syur nir-vraṇāni ca |
Manu06-53c/ teṣām adbhiḥ smṛtaṃ ;saucaṃ camasānām iva-adhvare ‖
Manu06-54a/ alābuṃ dārupātraṃ ca mṛṇmayaṃ vaidalaṃ tathā |
Manu06-54c/ etāṇi yatipātrāṇi manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo 'abravīt ‖
Manu06-55a/ ekakālaṃ cared bhaikṣaṃ na prasajjeta vistare |
Manu06-55c/ bhaikṣe prasakto hi yatir viṣayeṣv api sajjati ‖
Manu06-56a/ vi-dhūme sanna-musale vy-a:ngāre bhuktavaj-jane |
Manu06-56c/ vṛtte ;sarāvasaṃpāte bhikṣāṃ nityaṃ yati;s caret ‖
Manu06-57a/ alābhe na viṣadī syāt-lābhe ca-eva na harṣayet |
Manu06-57c/ prāṇayātrika-mātraḥ syāt- mātrāsa:ngād vinirgataḥ ‖
Manu06-58a/ abhipūjitalābhāṃs tu jugupseta-eva sarva;saḥ |
Manu06-58c/ abhipūjitalābhai;s ca yatir mukto 'api badhyate ‖
Manu06-59a/ alpānnābhyavahāreṇa rahaḥsthāna-āsanena ca |
Manu06-59c/ hriyamāṇāni viṣayair indriyāṇi nivartayet ‖
Manu06-60a/ indriyāṇāṃ nirodhena rāga-dve;sa-kṣayeṇa ca |
Manu06-60c/ ahiṃsayā ca bhūtānām amṛtatvāya kalpate ‖
Manu06-61a/ avekṣeta gatīr nṝṇāṃ karmadoṣa-samudbhavāḥ |
Manu06-61c/ niraye ca-eva patanaṃ yātanā;s ca yamakṣaye ‖
Manu06-62a/ viprayogaṃ priyai;s ca-eva saṃyogaṃ ca tathā-apriyaiḥ | 
Manu06-62c/ jarayā ca-abhibhavanaṃ vyādhibhi;s ca-upapīḍanaṃ ‖
Manu06-63a/ dehād utkramaṇaṃ ca-aṣmāt punar garbhe ca saṃbhavam |
Manu06-63c/ yonikoṭisahasreṣu sṛtī;s ca-asya-antarātmanaḥ ‖
Manu06-64a/ adharma-prabhavaṃ ca-eva duḥkhayogaṃ ;sarīriṇām |
Manu06-64c/ dharmārtha-prabhavaṃ ca-eva sukhasaṃyogam akṣayam ‖
Manu06-65a/ sūkṣmatāṃ ca-anvavekṣeta yogena paramātmanaḥ |
Manu06-65c/ deheṣu ca samutpattim uttameṣv adhameṣu ca ‖ [ṃ-deheṣu caivopapattim]
Manu06-66a/ dūṣito 'api cared dharmaṃ yatra tatra-ā;srame rataḥ | [ṃ-bhūṣito 'api ]
Manu06-66c/ samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu na li:ngaṃ dharmakāraṇam ‖
Manu06-67a/ phalaṃ katakavṛkṣasya yady apy ambuprasādakam |
Manu06-67c/ na nāmagrahaṇād eva tasya vāri prasīdati ‖
Manu06-68a/ saṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ jantūnāṃ rātrāv ahani vā sadā |
Manu06-68c/ ;sarīrasya-atyaye ca-eva samīkṣya vasudhāṃ caret ‖
Manu06-69a/ ahnā rātryā ca yā;n jantūn hinasty aj;nānato yatiḥ | 
Manu06-69c/ teṣāṃ snātvā vi;suddhyarthaṃ prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret ‖
Manu06-70a/ prāṇāyāmā brāhmaṇasya trayo 'api vidhivat kṛtāḥ |
Manu06-70c/ vyāhṛti-praṇavair yuktā vij;neyaṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ ‖
Manu06-71a/ dahyante dhmāyamānānāṃ dhātūnāṃ hi yathā malāḥ |
Manu06-71c/ tathā-indriyāṇāṃ dahyante doṣāḥ prāṇasya nigrahāt ‖
Manu06-72a/ prāṇāyamair dahed doṣān dhāraṇābhi;s ca kilbiṣam |
Manu06-72c/ pratyāhāreṇa saṃsargān dhyānena-an-ī;svarān guṇān ‖
Manu06-73a/ uccāvaceṣu bhūteṣu durj;neyām akṛta-ātmabhiḥ |
Manu06-73c/ dhyānayogena saṃpa;syed gatim asya-antarātmanaḥ ‖
Manu06-74a/ samyagdar;sanasaṃpannaḥ karmabhir na nibadhyate |
Manu06-74c/ dar;sanena vihīnas tu saṃsāraṃ pratipadyate ‖
Manu06-75a/ ahiṃsayā-indriya-a-sa:ngair vaidikai;s ca-eva karmabhiḥ |
Manu06-75c/ tapasa;s caraṇai;s ca-ugraiḥ sādhayanti-iha tatpadam ‖
Manu06-76a/ asthi-sthūṇaṃ snāyuyutaṃ māṃsa-;soṇita-lepanam |
Manu06-76c/ carmāvanaddhaṃ dur-gandhi pūrṇaṃ mūtra-purīṣayoḥ |
Manu06-77a/ jarā-;sokasamāviṣṭaṃ rogāyatanam āturam |
Manu06-77c/ rajasvalam anityaṃ ca bhūtāvāsam imaṃ tyajet ‖
Manu06-78a/ nadīkūlaṃ yathā vṛkṣo vṛkṣaṃ vā ;sakunir yathā |
Manu06-78c/ tathā tyajann imaṃ dehaṃ kṛcchrād grāhād vimucyate ‖
Manu06-79a/ priyeṣu sveṣu sukṛtam apriyeṣu ca duṣkṛtam |
Manu06-79c/ visṛjya dhyānayogena brahma-abhyeti sanātanam ‖
Manu06-80a/ yadā bhāvena bhavati sarvabhāveṣu niḥspṛhaḥ |
Manu06-80c/ tadā sukham avāpnoti pretya ca-iha ca ;sā;svatam ‖
Manu06-81a/ anena vidhinā sarvāṃs tyaktvā sa:ngān-;sanaiḥ ;sanaiḥ |
Manu06-81c/ sarvadvandvavinirmukto brahmaṇy eva-avatiṣṭhate ‖
Manu06-82a/ dhyānikaṃ sarvam eva-etad yad etad abhi;sabditam |
Manu06-82c/ na hy an-adhyātmavit ka;s cit kriyāphalam upā;snute ‖
Manu06-83a/ adhiyaj;naṃ brahma japed ādhidaivikam eva ca |
Manu06-83c/ ādhyātmikaṃ ca satataṃ vedāntābhihitaṃ ca yat ‖
Manu06-84a/ idaṃ ;saraṇam aj;nānām idam eva vijānatām |
Manu06-84c/ idam anvicchatāṃ svargam idam ānantyam icchatām ‖
Manu06-85a/ anena kramayogena parivrajati yo dvijaḥ |
Manu06-85c/ sa vidhūya-iha pāpmānaṃ paraṃ brahma-adhigacchati ‖

Manu06-86a/ eṣa dharmo 'anu;siṣṭo vo yatīnāṃ niyata-ātmanām |
Manu06-86c/ vedasaṃnyāsikānāṃ tu karmayogaṃ nibodhata ‖
Manu06-87a/ brahmacārī gṛhastha;s ca vānaprastho yatis tathā |
Manu06-87c/ ete gṛhastha-prabhavā;s catvāraḥ pṛthag ā;sramāḥ ‖
Manu06-88a/ sarve 'api krama;sas tv ete yathā;sāstraṃ niṣevitāḥ | 
Manu06-88c/ yathā-ukta-kāriṇaṃ vipraṃ nayanti paramāṃ gatim ‖
Manu06-89a/ sarveṣām api ca-eteṣāṃ veda-smṛtividhānataḥ | [ṃ-veda-;srutividhānataḥ ]
Manu06-89c/ gṛhastha ucyate ;sreṣṭhaḥ sa trīn etān bibharti hi ‖
Manu06-90a/ yathā nadī-nadāḥ sarve sāgare yānti saṃsthitim |
Manu06-90c/ tathā-eva-ā;sramiṇaḥ sarve gṛhasthe yānti saṃsthitim ‖
Manu06-91a/ caturbhir api ca-eva-etair nityam ā;sramibhir dvijaiḥ |
Manu06-91c/ da;sa-lakṣaṇako dharmaḥ sevitavyaḥ prayatnataḥ ‖
Manu06-92a/ dhṛtiḥ kṣamā damo 'asteyaṃ ;saucam indriyanigrahaḥ |
Manu06-92c/ dhīr vidyā satyam akrodho da;sakaṃ dharmalakṣaṇam ‖
Manu06-93a/ da;sa lakṣaṇāni dharmasya ye viprāḥ samadhīyate |
Manu06-93c/ adhītya ca-anuvartante te yānti paramāṃ gatim ‖
Manu06-94a/ da;sa-lakṣaṇakaṃ dharmam anutiṣṭhan samāhitaḥ |
Manu06-94c/ vedāntaṃ vidhivat-;srutvā saṃnyased an-ṛṇo dvijaḥ ‖
Manu06-95a/ saṃnyasya sarvakarmāṇi karmadoṣān apānudan |
Manu06-95c/ niyato vedam abhyasya putrai;svarye sukhaṃ vaset ‖
Manu06-96a/ evaṃ saṃnyasya karmāṇi svakārya-paramo 'a-spṛhaḥ |
Manu06-96c/ saṃnyāsena-apahatya-enaḥ prāpnoti paramaṃ gatim ‖
Manu06-97a/ eṣa vo 'abhihito dharmo brāhmaṇasya catur-vidhaḥ |
Manu06-97c/ puṇyo 'akṣaya-phalaḥ pretya rāj;nāṃ dharmaṃ nibodhata ‖


<B 7>

Manu07-01a/ rājadharmān pravakṣyāmi yathāvṛtto bhaven nṛpaḥ |
Manu07-01c/ saṃbhava;s ca yathā tasya siddhi;s ca paramā yathā ‖
Manu07-02a/ brāhmaṃ prāptena saṃskāraṃ kṣatriyeṇa yathāvidhi |
Manu07-02c/ sarvasya-asya yathānyāyaṃ kartavyaṃ parirakṣaṇam ‖
Manu07-03a/ a-rājake hi loke 'asmin sarvato vidruto bhayāt |
Manu07-03c/ rakṣārtham asya sarvasya rājānam asṛjat prabhuḥ ‖
Manu07-04a/ indra-anila-yama-arkāṇām agne;s ca varuṇasya ca |
Manu07-04c/ candra-vitte;sayo;s ca-eva mātrā nirhṛtya ;sā;svatīḥ ‖
Manu07-05a/ yasmād eṣāṃ surendrāṇāṃ mātrābhyo nirmito nṛpaḥ |
Manu07-05c/ tasmād abhibhavaty eṣa sarvabhūtāni tejasā ‖
Manu07-06a/ tapaty ādityavac ca-eṣa cakṣūṃṣi ca manāṃsi ca |
Manu07-06c/ na ca-enaṃ bhuvi ;saknoti ka;s cid apy abhivīkṣitum ‖
Manu07-07a/ so 'agnir bhavati vāyu;s ca so 'arkaḥ somaḥ sa dharmarāṭ |
Manu07-07c/ sa kuberaḥ sa varuṇaḥ sa mahendraḥ prabhāvataḥ ‖ [ṃ-sa ca-indraḥ svaprabhāvataḥ] ‖ [K: ]
Manu07-08a/ bālo 'api na-avamāntavyo manuṣya iti bhūmipaḥ |
Manu07-08c/ mahatī devatā hy eṣā nararūpeṇa tiṣṭhati ‖
Manu07-09a/ ekam eva dahaty agnir naraṃ durupasarpiṇam |
Manu07-09c/ kulaṃ dahati rājā-agniḥ sa-pa;su-dravyasaṃcayam ‖
Manu07-10a/ kāryaṃ so 'avekṣya ;saktiṃ ca de;sa-kālau ca tattvataḥ |
Manu07-10c/ kurute dharmasiddhyarthaṃ vi;svarūpaṃ punaḥ punaḥ ‖
Manu07-11a/ yasya prasāde padmā ;srīr vijaya;s ca parākrame |
Manu07-11c/ mṛtyu;s ca vasati krodhe sarvatejomayo hi saḥ ‖
Manu07-12a/ taṃ yas tu dveṣṭi saṃmohāt sa vina;syaty asaṃ;sayam |
Manu07-12c/ tasya hy ā;su vinā;sāya rājā prakurute manaḥ ‖
Manu07-13a/ tasmād dharmaṃ yam iṣṭeṣu sa vyavasyen narādhipaḥ |
Manu07-13c/ aniṣṭaṃ ca-apy aniṣṭeṣu taṃ dharmaṃ na vicālayet ‖

Manu07-14a/ tasyārthe sarvabhūtānāṃ goptāraṃ dharmam ātmajam | [ṃ-tadarthaṃ]
Manu07-14c/ brahmatejomayaṃ daṇḍam asṛjat pūrvam ī;svaraḥ ‖
Manu07-15a/ tasya sarvāṇi bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca |
Manu07-15c/ bhayād bhogāya kalpante svadharmāt-na calanti ca ‖
Manu07-16a/ taṃ de;sa-kālau ;saktiṃ ca vidyāṃ ca-avekṣya tattvataḥ |
Manu07-16c/ yathārhataḥ saṃpraṇayen nareṣv anyāya-vartiṣu ‖
Manu07-17a/ sa rājā puruṣo daṇḍaḥ sa netā ;sāsitā ca saḥ |
Manu07-17c/ caturṇām ā;sramāṇāṃ ca dharmasya pratibhūḥ smṛtaḥ ‖
Manu07-18a/ daṇḍaḥ ;sāsti prajāḥ sarvā daṇḍa eva-abhirakṣati |
Manu07-18c/ daṇḍaḥ supteṣu jāgarti daṇḍaṃ dharmaṃ vidur budhāḥ ‖
Manu07-19a/ samīkṣya sa dhṛtaḥ samyak sarvā ra;njayati prajāḥ |
Manu07-19c/ a-samīkṣya praṇītas tu vinā;sayati sarvataḥ ‖
Manu07-20a/ yadi na praṇayed rājā daṇḍaṃ daṇḍyeṣv atandritaḥ |
Manu07-20c/ ;sūle matsyān iva-apakṣyan durbalān balavattarāḥ ‖
Manu07-21a/ adyāt kākaḥ puroḍā;saṃ  ;svā ca lihyādd havis tathā | [ṃ-;svā-avalihyādd]
Manu07-21c/ svāmyaṃ ca na syāt kasmiṃ;s cit pravarteta-adhara-uttaram ‖
Manu07-22a/ sarvo daṇḍajito loko durlabho hi ;sucir naraḥ |
Manu07-22c/ daṇḍasya hi bhayāt sarvaṃ jagad bhogāya kalpate ‖
Manu07-23a/ deva-dānava-gandharvā rakṣāṃsi pataga-uragāḥ |
Manu07-23c/ te 'api bhogāya kalpante daṇḍena-eva nipīḍitāḥ ‖
Manu07-24a/ duṣyeyuḥ sarvavarṇā;s ca bhidyeran sarvasetavaḥ |
Manu07-24c/ sarvalokaprakopa;s ca bhaved daṇḍasya vibhramāt ‖
Manu07-25a/ yatra ;syāmo lohita-akṣo daṇḍa;s carati pāpahā |
Manu07-25c/ prajās tatra na muhyanti netā cet sādhu pa;syati ‖
Manu07-26a/ tasya-āhuḥ saṃpraṇetāraṃ rājānaṃ satyavādinam |
Manu07-26c/ samīkṣyakāriṇaṃ prāj;naṃ dharma-kāma-artha-kovidam ‖
Manu07-27a/ taṃ rājā praṇayan samyak trivargeṇa-abhivardhate |
Manu07-27c/ kāma-ātmā viṣamaḥ kṣudro daṇḍena-eva nihanyate ‖ [ṃ-kāma-andho ]
Manu07-28a/ daṇḍo hi sumahat-tejo durdhara;s ca-akṛta-ātmabhiḥ |
Manu07-28c/ dharmād vicalitaṃ hanti nṛpam eva sa-bāndhavam ‖
Manu07-29a/ tato durgaṃ ca rāṣṭraṃ ca lokaṃ ca sa-cara-acaram |
Manu07-29c/ antarikṣagatāṃ;s ca-eva munīn devāṃ;s ca pīḍayet ‖
Manu07-30a/ so 'asahāyena mūḍhena lubdhena-akṛta-buddhinā |
Manu07-30c/ na ;sakyo nyāyato netuṃ saktena viṣayeṣu ca ‖
Manu07-31a/ ;sucinā satyasaṃdhena yathā;sāstra-anusāriṇā |
Manu07-31c/ praṇetuṃ ;sakyate daṇḍaḥ susahāyena dhīmatā ‖
Manu07-32a/ svarāṣṭre nyāyavṛttaḥ syād bhṛ;sa-daṇḍa;s ca ;satruṣu |
Manu07-32c/ suhṛtsv ajihmaḥ snigdheṣu brāhmaṇeṣu kṣamānvitaḥ ‖
Manu07-33a/ evaṃvṛttasya nṛpateḥ ;silā-u;nchena-api jīvataḥ |
Manu07-33c/ vistīryate ya;so loke tailabindur iva-ambhasi ‖
Manu07-34a/ atas tu viparītasya nṛpater ajita-ātmanaḥ |
Manu07-34c/ saṃkṣipyate ya;so loke ghṛtabindur iva-ambhasi ‖
Manu07-35a/ sve sve dharme niviṣṭānāṃ sarveṣām anupūrva;saḥ |
Manu07-35c/ varṇānām ā;sramāṇāṃ ca rājā sṛṣṭo 'abhirakṣitā ‖
Manu07-36a/ tena yad yat sa-bhṛtyena kartavyaṃ rakṣatā prajāḥ |
Manu07-36c/ tat tad vo 'ahaṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāvad anupūrva;saḥ ‖

Manu07-37a/ brāhmaṇān paryupāsīta prātar utthāya pārthivaḥ |
Manu07-37c/ traividyavṛddhān viduṣas tiṣṭhet teṣāṃ ca ;sāsane ‖
Manu07-38a/ vṛddhāṃ;s ca nityaṃ seveta viprān vedavidaḥ ;sucīn |
Manu07-38c/ vṛddhasevī hi satataṃ rakṣobhir api pūjyate ‖
Manu07-39a/ tebhyo 'adhigacched vinayaṃ vinīta-ātmā-api nitya;saḥ |
Manu07-39c/ vinīta-ātmā hi nṛpatir na vina;syati karhi cit ‖
Manu07-40a/ bahavo 'avinayāt- naṣṭā rājānaḥ sa-paricchadāḥ |[ṃ-sa-parigrahāḥ]
Manu07-40c/ vanasthā api rājyāni vinayāt pratipedire ‖
Manu07-41a/ veno vinaṣṭo 'avinayāt-nahuṣa;s ca-eva pārthivaḥ |
Manu07-41c/ sudāḥ paijavana;s ca-eva sumukho nimir eva ca ‖
Manu07-42a/ pṛthus tu vinayād rājyaṃ prāptavān manur eva ca |
Manu07-42c/ kubera;s ca dhanai;svaryaṃ brāhmaṇyaṃ ca-eva gādhijaḥ ‖
Manu07-43a/ traividyebhyas trayīṃ vidyāṃ daṇḍanītiṃ ca ;sā;svatīm | [ṃ-trayīṃ vidyāt ]
Manu07-43c/ ānvīkṣikīṃ ca-ātmavidyāṃ vārtārambhāṃ;s ca lokataḥ ‖
Manu07-44a/ indriyāṇāṃ jaye yogaṃ samātiṣṭhed divā-ni;sam |
Manu07-44c/ jita-indriyo hi ;saknoti va;se sthāpayituṃ prajāḥ ‖
Manu07-45a/ da;sa kāma-samutthāni tathā-aṣṭau krodhajāni ca |
Manu07-45c/ vyasanāni dur-antāni prayatnena vivarjayet ‖
Manu07-46a/ kāmajeṣu prasakto hi vyasaneṣu mahīpatiḥ |
Manu07-46c/ viyujyate 'artha-dharmābhyāṃ krodhajeṣv ātmanā-eva tu ‖
Manu07-47a/ mṛgayā-akṣo divāsvapnaḥ parivādaḥ striyo madaḥ |
Manu07-47c/ tauryatrikaṃ vṛthāṭyā ca kāmajo da;sako gaṇaḥ ‖
Manu07-48a/ pai;sunyaṃ sāhasaṃ droha īrṣyā-asūyā-arthadūṣaṇam |
Manu07-48c/ vāgdaṇḍajaṃ ca pāruṣyaṃ krodhajo 'api gaṇo 'aṣṭakaḥ ‖
Manu07-49a/ dvayor apy etayor mūlaṃ yaṃ sarve kavayo viduḥ |
Manu07-49c/ taṃ yatnena jayet-lobhaṃ tajjāv etāv ubhau gaṇau ‖
Manu07-50a/ pānam akṣāḥ striya;s ca-eva mṛgayā ca yathākramam |
Manu07-50c/ etat kaṣṭatamaṃ vidyāt-catuṣkaṃ kāmaje gaṇe ‖
Manu07-51a/ daṇḍasya pātanaṃ ca-eva vākpāruṣya-arthadūṣaṇe |
Manu07-51c/ krodhaje 'api gaṇe vidyāt kaṣṭam etat trikaṃ sadā ‖
Manu07-52a/ saptakasya-asya vargasya sarvatra-eva-anuṣa:ngiṇaḥ |
Manu07-52c/ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ gurutaraṃ vidyād vyasanam ātmavān |
Manu07-53a/ vyasanasya ca mṛtyo;s ca vyasanaṃ kaṣṭam ucyate |
Manu07-53c/ vyasany adho 'adho vrajati svar yāty avyasanī mṛtaḥ ‖
Manu07-54a/ maulān-;sāstravidaḥ ;sūrān-labdha-lakṣān kula-udbhavān | [ṃ-kula-udgatān ]
Manu07-54c/ sacivān sapta ca-aṣṭau vā prakurvīta parīkṣitān ‖ [ṃ-kurvīta suparīkṣitān]
Manu07-55a/ api yat sukaraṃ karma tad apy ekena duṣkaram |
Manu07-55c/ vi;seṣato 'asahāyena kiṃ tu  rājyaṃ mahā-udayam ‖ [ṃ-kiṃ nu ]
Manu07-56a/ taiḥ sārdhaṃ cintayen nityaṃ sāmānyaṃ saṃdhi-vigraham |
Manu07-56c/ sthānaṃ samudayaṃ guptiṃ labdhapra;samanāni ca ‖
Manu07-57a/ teṣāṃ svaṃ svam abhiprāyam upalabhya pṛthak pṛthak |
Manu07-57c/ samastānāṃ ca kāryeṣu vidadhyādd hitam ātmanaḥ ‖
Manu07-58a/ sarveṣāṃ tu vi;siṣṭena brāhmaṇena vipa;scitā |
Manu07-58c/ mantrayet paramaṃ mantraṃ rājā ṣāḍguṇyasaṃyutam ‖
Manu07-59a/ nityaṃ tasmin samā;svastaḥ sarvakāryāṇi niḥkṣipet  | [ṃ-nikṣipet]
Manu07-59c/ tena sārdhaṃ vini;scitya tataḥ karma samārabhet ‖
Manu07-60a/ anyān api prakurvīta ;sucīn prāj;nān avasthitān |
Manu07-60c/ samyag arthasamāhartṝn amātyān suparīkṣitān ‖
Manu07-61a/ nirvarteta-asya yāvadbhir itikartavyatā nṛbhiḥ |
Manu07-61c/ tāvato 'atandritān dakṣān prakurvīta vicakṣaṇān ‖
Manu07-62a/ teṣām arthe niyu;njīta ;sūrān dakṣān kula-udgatān |
Manu07-62c/ ;sucīn ākara-karmānte bhīrūn antarnive;sane ‖
Manu07-63a/ dūtaṃ ca-eva prakurvīta sarva;sāstra-vi;sāradam |
Manu07-63c/ i:ngita-ākāra-ceṣṭaj;naṃ ;suciṃ dakṣaṃ kula-udgatam ‖
Manu07-64a/ anuraktaḥ ;sucir dakṣaḥ smṛtimān de;sa-kālavit |
Manu07-64c/ vapuṣmān vītabhīr vāgmī dūto rāj;naḥ pra;sasyate ‖
Manu07-65a/ amātye daṇḍa āyatto daṇḍe vainayikī kriyā |
Manu07-65c/ nṛpatau ko;sa-rāṣṭre ca dūte saṃdhi-viparyayau ‖
Manu07-66a/ dūta eva hi saṃdhatte bhinatty eva ca saṃhatān |
Manu07-66c/ dūtas tat kurute karma bhidyante yena mānavaḥ ‖
Manu07-67a/ sa vidyād asya kṛtyeṣu nirgūḍha-i:ngita-ceṣṭitaiḥ |
Manu07-67c/ ākāram i:ngitaṃ ceṣṭāṃ bhṛtyeṣu ca cikīrṣitam ‖
Manu07-68a/ buddhvā ca sarvaṃ tattvena pararājacikīrṣitam |
Manu07-68c/ tathā prayatnam ātiṣṭhed yathā-ātmānaṃ na pīḍayet ‖
Manu07-69a/ jā:ngalaṃ sasyasaṃpannam ārya-prāyam an-āvilam |
Manu07-69c/ ramyam ānata-sāmantaṃ svājīvyaṃ de;sam āvaset ‖
Manu07-70a/ dhanva-durgaṃ mahī-durgam ab-durgaṃ vārkṣam eva vā |
Manu07-70c/ nṛdurgaṃ giridurgaṃ vā samā;sritya vaset puram ‖
Manu07-71a/ sarveṇa tu prayatnena giridurgaṃ samā;srayet |
Manu07-71c/ eṣāṃ hi bāhuguṇyena giridurgaṃ vi;siṣyate ‖
Manu07-72a/ triṇy ādyāny ā;sritās tv eṣāṃ mṛga-gartā;sraya-apcarāḥ | 
Manu07-72c/ trīṇy uttarāṇi krama;saḥ plavaṃgama-nara-amarāḥ ‖
Manu07-73a/ yathā durgā;sritān etān na-upahiṃsanti ;satravaḥ |
Manu07-73c/ tathā-arayo na hiṃsanti nṛpaṃ durgasamā;sritam ‖
Manu07-74a/ ekaḥ ;sataṃ yodhayati prākārastho dhanurdharaḥ |
Manu07-74c/ ;sataṃ da;sasahasrāṇi tasmād durgaṃ vidhīyate ‖
Manu07-75a/ tat syād āyudhasaṃpannaṃ dhana-dhānyena vāhanaiḥ |
Manu07-75c/ brāhmaṇaiḥ ;silpibhir yantrair yavasena-udakena ca ‖
Manu07-76a/ tasya madhye suparyāptaṃ kārayed gṛham ātmanaḥ |
Manu07-76c/ guptaṃ sarva-ṛtukaṃ ;subhraṃ jala-vṛkṣasamanvitam ‖
Manu07-77a/ tad adhyāsya-udvahed bhāryāṃ sa-varṇāṃ lakṣaṇānvitām |
Manu07-77c/ kule mahati saṃbhūtāṃ hṛdyāṃ rūpa-guṇānvītām ‖
Manu07-78a/ purohitaṃ ca kurvīta vṛṇuyād eva ca-ṛtvijaḥ |
Manu07-78c/ te 'asya gṛhyāṇi karmāṇi kuryur vaitānikāni ca ‖
Manu07-79a/ yajeta rājā kratubhir vividhair āpta-dakṣiṇaiḥ |
Manu07-79c/ dharmārthaṃ ca-eva viprebhyo dadyād bhogān dhanāni ca ‖
Manu07-80a/ sāṃvatsarikam āptai;s ca rāṣṭrād āhārayed balim |
Manu07-80c/ syāc ca-āmnāya-paro loke varteta pitṛvat-nṛṣu ‖
Manu07-81a/ adhyakṣān vividhān kuryāt tatra tatra vipa;scitaḥ |
Manu07-81c/ te 'asya sarvāṇy avekṣeran nṛṇāṃ kāryāṇi kurvatām ‖
Manu07-82a/ āvṛttānāṃ gurukulād viprāṇāṃ pūjako bhavet |
Manu07-82c/ nṛpāṇām akṣayo hy eṣa nidhir brāhmo 'abhidhīyate ‖
Manu07-83a/ na taṃ stenā na ca-amitrā haranti na ca na;syati |
Manu07-83c/ tasmād rāj;nā nidhātavyo brāhmaṇeṣv akṣayo nidhiḥ ‖
Manu07-84a/ na skandate na vyathate na vina;syati karhi cit |[ṃ- na skandati na cyavate] 
Manu07-84c/ variṣṭham agnihotrebhyo brāhmaṇasya mukhe hutam ‖
Manu07-85a/ samam abrāhmaṇe dānaṃ dviguṇaṃ brāhmaṇabruve |
Manu07-85c/ prādhīte ;satasāhasram anantaṃ vedapārage ‖ [ṃ-ācārye ;satasāhasram]] [;n: sahasraguṇam ācārye]
Manu07-86a/ pātrasya hi vi;seṣeṇa ;sraddadhānatayā-eva ca |
Manu07-86c/ alpaṃ vā bahu vā pretya dānasya phalam a;snute ‖
[Manu07-87ṃa/ de;sa-kālavidhānena dravyaṃ ;sraddhāsamanvitam / [not in K]
[Manu07-87ṃc/ pātre pradīyate) yat tu tad dharmasya prasādhanam // [not in K]

Manu07-87a[88ṃa]/ sama-uttama-adhamai rājā tv āhūtaḥ pālayan prajāḥ |
Manu07-87c[88ṃc]/ na nivarteta saṃgrāmāt kṣātraṃ dharmam anusmaran ‖
Manu07-88a[89ṃa]/ saṃgrāmeṣv anivartitvaṃ prajānāṃ ca-eva pālanam |
Manu07-88c[89ṃc]/ ;su;srūṣā brāhmaṇānāṃ ca rāj;nāṃ ;sreyaskaraṃ param ‖
Manu07-89a[90ṃa]/ āhaveṣu mitho 'anyonyaṃ jighāṃsanto mahīkṣitaḥ |
Manu07-89c[90ṃc]/ yudhyamānāḥ paraṃ ;saktyā svargaṃ yānty a-parā:nmukhāḥ ‖
Manu07-90a[91ṃa]/ na kūṭair āyudhair hanyād yudhyamāno raṇe ripūn |
Manu07-90c[91ṃc]/ na karṇibhir na-api digdhair na-agnijvalita-tejanaiḥ ‖
Manu07-91a[92ṃa]/ na ca hanyāt sthalārūḍhaṃ na klībaṃ na kṛta-a;njalim |
Manu07-91c[92ṃc]/ na mukta-ke;saṃ na-āsīnaṃ na tava-asmi-iti vādinam ‖

Manu07-93a[94ṃa]/ na-āyudhavyasanaprāptaṃ na-ārtaṃ na-atiparikṣataṃ |
Manu07-93c[94ṃc]/ na bhītaṃ na parāvṛttaṃ satāṃ dharmam anusmaran ‖

Manu07-99c[100ṃc]/ rakṣitaṃ vardhayec ca-eva vṛddhaṃ pātreṣu nikṣipet ‖

<B 8>

Manu08-01a/ vyavahārān didṛkṣus tu brāhmaṇaiḥ saha pārthivaḥ |
Manu08-01c/ mantraj;nair mantribhi;s ca-eva vinītaḥ pravi;set) sabhām ‖
Manu08-02a/ tatra-āsīnaḥ sthito vā-api pāṇim udyamya) dakṣiṇam |
Manu08-02c/ vinīta-veṣa-ābharaṇaḥ pa;syet) kāryāṇi kāryiṇām ‖
Manu08-03a/ pratyahaṃ de;sadṛṣṭai;s ca ;sāstradṛṣṭai;s ca hetubhiḥ |
Manu08-03c/ aṣṭāda;sasu mārgeṣu nibaddhāni pṛthak pṛthak ‖

Manu08-99a/ hanti) jātān ajātāṃ;s ca hiraṇyārthe 'anṛtaṃ vadan) |
Manu08-99c/ sarvaṃ bhūmi-anṛte hanti) mā sma bhūmi-anṛtaṃ vadīḥ) ‖
Manu08-100a/ apsu bhūmivad ity āhuḥ) strīṇāṃ bhoge ca maithune |
Manu08-100c/ abjeṣu ca-eva ratneṣu sarveṣv a;smamayeṣu ca ‖
Manu08-101a/ etān doṣān avekṣya) tvaṃ sarvān anṛtabhāṣaṇe |
Manu08-101c/ yathā;srutaṃ yathādṛṣṭaṃ sarvam eva-a;njasā vada) ‖
Manu08-102a/ gorakṣakān vāṇijikāṃs  tathā kāru-ku;sīlavān | [ṃ-vāṇijakāṃs]
Manu08-102c/ preṣyān vārdhuṣikāṃ;s ca-eva viprān ;sūdravad ācaret) ‖

Manu08-103a/ tad vadan dharmato 'artheṣu jānann) apy anythā naraḥ |
Manu08-103c/ na svargāc cyavate) lokād daivīṃ vācaṃ vadanti) tām ‖
Manu08-104a/ ;sūdra-vi;s-kṣatra-viprāṇāṃ yatra-ṛta-uktau bhaved) vadhaḥ |
Manu08-104c/ tatra vaktavyam anṛtaṃ tadd hi satyād vi;siṣyate) ‖
Manu08-105a/ vāc-daivatyai;s ca carubhir yajeraṃs) te sarasvatīm |
Manu08-105c/ anṛtasya-enasas tasya kurvāṇā) niṣkṛtiṃ parām ‖
Manu08-106a/ kūṣmāṇḍair vā-api juhuyād) ghṛtam agnau yathāvidhi | [ṭA10-3-5; Vṣ20-14]
Manu08-106c/ ud ity ṛcā vā vāruṇyā tṛcena-ap-daivatena vā ‖ [ṛṣ1-24-15; 10-9-1-3; Vṣ12-2;Vṣ12-50]

Manu08-107a/ tripakṣād abruvan sākṣyam ṛṇādiṣu naro 'a-gadaḥ |
Manu08-107c/ tadṛṇaṃ prāpnuyāt) sarvaṃ da;sabandhaṃ ca sarvataḥ ‖
Manu08-108a/ yasya dṛ;syeta) saptāhād ukta-vākyasya sākṣiṇaḥ |
Manu08-108c/ rogo 'agnir j;nātimaraṇam ṛṇaṃ dāpyo) damaṃ ca saḥ ‖

Manu08-109a/ asākṣikeṣu tv artheṣu mitho vivādamānayoḥ) |
Manu08-109c/ avindaṃs) tattvataḥ satyaṃ ;sapathena-api lambhayet) ‖
Manu08-110a/ maharṣibhi;s ca devai;s ca kāryārthaṃ ;sapathāḥ kṛtāḥ) |
Manu08-110c/ vasiṣṭha;s ca-api ;sapathaṃ ;sepe) paijavane nṛpe ‖
Manu08-111a/ na vṛthā ;sapathaṃ kuryāt) svalpe 'apy arthe naro budhaḥ |
Manu08-111c/ vṛthā hi ;sapathaṃ kurvan) pretya ca-iha ca na;syati) |
Manu08-112a/ kāminīṣu vivāheṣu gavāṃ bhakṣye tathā-indhane |
Manu08-112c/ brāhmaṇa-abhyupapattau ca ;sapathe na-asti) pātakam ‖
Manu08-113a/ satyena ;sāpayed) vipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhana-āyudhaiḥ |
Manu08-113c/ go-bīja-kā;ncanair vai;syaṃ ;sūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ ‖
Manu08-114a/ agniṃ vā-āhārayed) enam apsu ca-enaṃ nimajjayet) |
Manu08-114c/ putra-dārasya vā-apy enaṃ ;sirāṃsi spar;sayet) pṛthak ‖
Manu08-115a/ yam iddho) na dahaty) agnir āpo na-unmajjayanti) ca |
Manu08-115c/ na ca-ārtim ṛcchati) kṣipraṃ sa j;neyaḥ) ;sapathe ;suciḥ ‖
Manu08-116a/ vatsasya hy abhi;sastasya) purā bhrātrā yavīyasā |
Manu08-116c/ na-agnir dadāha) roma-api satyena jagataḥ spa;saḥ ‖

Manu08-117a/ yasmin yasmin vivāde tu kauṭasākṣyaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet) |
Manu08-117c/ tat tat kāryaṃ nivarteta) kṛtaṃ ca-apy akṛtaṃ bhavet) ‖
Manu08-118a/ lobhāt-mohād bhayāt-maitrāt kāmāt krodhāt tathā-eva ca |
Manu08-118c/ aj;nānād bālabhāvāt-ca sākṣyaṃ vitatham ucyate) |
Manu08-119a/ eṣām anyatame sthāne yaḥ sākṣyam anṛtaṃ vadet) |
Manu08-119c/ tasya daṇḍavi;seṣāṃs tu pravakṣyāmy) anupūrva;saḥ ‖
Manu08-120a/ lobhāt sahasraṃ daṇḍyas tu mohāt pūrvaṃ tu sāhasam |
Manu08-120c/ bhayād dvau madhyamau daṇḍau maitrāt pūrvaṃ caturguṇam ‖
Manu08-121a/ kāmād da;saguṇaṃ pūrvaṃ krodhāt tu triguṇaṃ param |
Manu08-121c/ aj;nānād dve ;sate pūrṇe bāli;syāt-;satam eva tu ‖
Manu08-122a/ etān āhuḥ) kauṭasākṣye proktān) daṇḍān manīṣibhiḥ |
Manu08-122c/ dharmasya-avyabhicārārtham adharmaniyamāya ca ‖
Manu08-123a/ kauṭasākṣyaṃ tu kurvāṇāṃs) trīn varṇān dhārmiko nṛpaḥ |
Manu08-123c/ pravāsayed) daṇḍayitvā) brāhmaṇaṃ tu vivāsayet) ‖

Manu08-124a/ da;sa sthānāni daṇḍasya manuḥ svayaṃbhuvo 'abravīt) |
Manu08-124c/ triṣu varṇeṣu yāni syur) akṣato brāhmaṇo vrajet) ‖
Manu08-125a/ upastham udaraṃ jihvā hastau pādau ca pa;ncamam |
Manu08-125c/ cakṣur nāsā ca karṇau ca dhanaṃ dehas tathā-eva ca ‖
Manu08-126a/ anubandhaṃ parij;nāya) de;sa-kālau ca tattvataḥ |
Manu08-126c/ sāra-aparādho ca-ālokya) daṇḍaṃ daṇḍyeṣu pātayet) ‖
Manu08-127a/ adharmadaṇḍanaṃ loke ya;soghnaṃ kīrtinā;sanam |
Manu08-127c/ asvargyaṃ ca paratra-api tasmāt tat parivarjayet) ‖
Manu08-128a/ adaṇḍyān daṇḍayan) rājā daṇḍyāṃ;s ca-eva-apy adaṇḍayan) |
Manu08-128c/ aya;so mahad āpnoti) narakaṃ ca-eva gacchati) ‖
Manu08-129a/ vāgdaṇḍaṃ prathamaṃ kuryād) dhigdaṇḍaṃ tadanantaram |
Manu08-129c/ tṛtīyaṃ dhanadaṇḍaṃ tu vadhadaṇḍam ataḥ param ‖
Manu08-130a/ vadhena-api yadā tv etān nigrahītuṃ) na ;saknuyāt) |
Manu08-130c/ tadā-eṣu sarvam apy etat prayu;njīta) catuṣṭayam ‖

Manu08-131a/ lokasaṃvyavahārārthaṃ yāḥ saṃj;nāḥ prathitā) bhuvi |
Manu08-131c/ tāmra-rūpya-suvarṇānāṃ tāḥ pravakṣyāmy) a;seṣataḥ ‖

Manu08-141a/ dvikaṃ ;sataṃ vā gṛhṇīyāt) satāṃ dharmam anusmaran) |
Manu08-141c/ dvikaṃ ;sataṃ hi gṛhṇāno) na bhavaty) arthakilbiṣī ‖
Manu08-142a/ dvikaṃ trikaṃ catuṣkaṃ ca pa;ncakaṃ ca ;sataṃ samam |
Manu08-142c/ māsasya vṛddhiṃ gṛhṇīyād) varṇānām anupūrva;saḥ ‖
Manu08-143a/ na tv eva-ādhau sa-upakāre kausīdīṃ vṛddhim āpnuyāt) |

Manu08-144c/ mūlyena toṣayec) ca-enam ādhisteno 'anyathā bhavet) ‖

Manu08-145a/ ādhi;s ca-upanidhi;s ca-ubhau na kālātyayam arhataḥ) |

Manu08-152c/ kusīdapatham āhus) taṃ pa;ncakaṃ ;satam arhati) ‖
Manu08-153a/ na-ati-sāṃvatsarīṃ vṛddhiṃ na ca-adṛṣṭāṃ  punar haret | [ṃ-vinirharet)]

Manu08-154a/ ṛṇaṃ dātum a;sakto yaḥ kartum icchet) punaḥ kriyām |

Manu08-179a/ kulaje vṛttasaṃpanne dharmaj;ne satyavādini |

Manu08-180c/ sa tathā-eva grahītavyo) yathā dāyas tathā grahaḥ ‖

Manu08-192c/ tathā-upanidhihartāram avi;seṣeṇa pārthivaḥ ‖
Manu08-193a/ upadhābhi;s ca yaḥ ka;s cit paradravyaṃ haren) naraḥ |
Manu08-193c/ sa-sahāyaḥ sa hantavyaḥ) prakā;saṃ vividhair vadhaiḥ ‖
Manu08-194a/ nikṣepo yaḥ kṛto) yena yāvāṃ;s ca kulasaṃnidhau |
Manu08-194c/ tāvān eva sa vij;neyo) vibruvan) daṇḍam arhati) ‖
Manu08-195a/ mitho dāyaḥ kṛto) yena gṛhīto) mitha eva vā |
Manu08-195c/ mitha eva pradātavyo) yathā dāyas tathā grahaḥ ‖
Manu08-196a/ nikṣiptasya) dhanasya-evaṃ prītyā-upanihitasya) ca |
Manu08-196c/ rājā vinirṇayaṃ kuryād) akṣiṇvan) nyāsadhāriṇam ‖

Manu08-197a/ vikrīṇīte) parasya svaṃ yo 'asvāmī svāmyasammataḥ) |
Manu08-197c/ na taṃ nayeta) sākṣyaṃ tu stenam astenamāninam ‖
Manu08-198a/ avahāryo bhavet)-ca-eva sa-anvayaḥ ṣaṭ;sataṃ damam |
Manu08-198c/ nir-anvayo 'an-apasaraḥ prāptaḥ) syāc) caurakilbiṣam ‖
Manu08-199a/ asvāminā kṛto yas tu dāyo vikraya eva vā |
Manu08-199c/ akṛtaḥ sa tu vij;neyo) vyavahāre yathā sthitiḥ ‖
Manu08-200a/ saṃbhogo dṛ;syate) yatra na dṛ;syeta)-āgamaḥ kva cit |
Manu08-200c/ āgamaḥ kāraṇaṃ tatra na saṃbhoga iti sthitiḥ ‖
Manu08-201a/ vikrayād yo dhanaṃ kiṃ cid gṛhṇīyat) kulasaṃnidhau |
Manu08-201c/ krayeṇa sa vi;suddhaṃ hi nyāyato labhate) dhanam ‖
Manu08-202a/ atha mūlam anāhāryaṃ prakā;sakraya;sodhitaḥ) |
Manu08-202c/ adaṇḍyo) mucyate) rāj;nā nāṣṭiko labhate) dhanam ‖
Manu08-203a/ na-anyad anyena saṃsṛṣṭarūpaṃ vikrayam arhati) |
Manu08-203c/ na ca-a-sāraṃ na ca nyūnaṃ na dūreṇa tirohitam ‖ [ṃ- na sāvadyaṃ na ca nyūnaṃ na dūre na tirohitam ] 
Manu08-204a/ anyāṃ ced dar;sayitvā)-anyā voḍhuḥ kanyā pradīyate) |
Manu08-204c/ ubhe ta eka;sulkena vahed) ity abravīn) manuḥ ‖
Manu08-205a/ na-unmattāyā na kuṣṭhinyā na ca yā spṛṣṭa-maithunā |
Manu08-205c/ pūrvaṃ doṣān abhikhyāpya) pradātā daṇḍam arhati) ‖

Manu08-206a/ ṛtvig yadi vṛto) yaj;ne svakarma parihāpayet) |
Manu08-206c/ tasya karmānurūpeṇa deyo) 'aṃ;saḥ sahakartṛbhiḥ ‖
Manu08-207a/ dakṣiṇāsu ca dattāsu) svakarma parihāpayan) |
Manu08-207c/ kṛtsnam eva labheta)-aṃ;sam anyena-eva ca kārayet) ‖
Manu08-208a/ yasmin karmaṇi yās tu syur) uktāḥ pratya:ngadakṣiṇāḥ |
Manu08-208c/ sa eva tā ādidīta) bhajeran) sarva eva vā ‖
Manu08-209a/ rathaṃ haret) ca-adhvaryur brahmā-ādhāne ca vājinam |
Manu08-209c/ hotā vā-api hared) a;svam udgātā ca-apy anaḥ kraye ‖
Manu08-210a/ sarveṣām ardhino mukhyās tadardhena-ardhino 'apare |
Manu08-210c/ tṛtīyinas tṛtīyāṃ;sā;s caturthāṃ;sā;s ca pādinaḥ ‖
Manu08-211a/ saṃbhūya) svāni karmāṇi kurvadbhir) iha mānavaiḥ |
Manu08-211c/ anena vidhiyogena kartavyā)-aṃ;saprakalpanā ‖

Manu08-212a/ dharmārthaṃ yena dattaṃ syāt) kasmai cid yācate) dhanam |
Manu08-212c/ pa;scāc ca na tathā tat syān) na deyaṃ tasya tad bhavet) ‖
Manu08-213a/ yadi saṃsādhayet) tat tu darpāt-lobhena vā punaḥ |
Manu08-213c/ rāj;nā dāpyaḥ) suvarṇaṃ syāt) tasya steyasya niṣkṛtiḥ ‖
Manu08-214a/ dattasya)-eṣā-uditā) dharmyā yathāvad anapakriyā |
Manu08-214c/ ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi) vetanasya-anapakriyām ‖

Manu08-215a/ bhṛto na-ārto na kuryād) yo darpāt karma yathāa-uditam | [ṃ-anārto ]
Manu08-215c/ sa daṇḍyaḥ) kṛṣṇalāny aṣṭau na deyaṃ) ca-asya vetanam ‖
Manu08-216a/ ārtas tu kuryāt) svasthaḥ san) yathābhāṣitam āditaḥ |
Manu08-216c/ sa dīrghasya-api kālasya tat-labheta(-eva vetanam ‖ 
Manu08-217a/ yathā-uktam ārtaḥ sustho vā yas tat karma na kārayet) |

Manu09-70c/ mitho bhajeta)-ā prasavāt sakṛt-sakṛd ṛtāv-ṛtau ‖

Manu09-72a/ vidhivat pratigṛhya)-api tyajet) kanyāṃ vigarhitām |

Manu09-74a/ vidhāya) vṛttiṃ bhāryāyāḥ pravaset) kāryavān naraḥ |

Manu09-76c/ vidyārthaṃ ṣaḍ ya;so-'arthaṃ vā kāmārthaṃ trīṃs tu vatsarān ‖

Manu09-77a/ saṃvatsaraṃ pratīkṣeta) dviṣantīṃ yoṣitaṃ patiḥ | [ṃ-dviṣāṇāṃ]

Manu10-25a/ saṃkīrṇa-yonayo ye tu pratiloma-anuloma-jāḥ |
Manu10-25c/ anyonyavyatiṣaktā;s ca tān pravakṣyāmy) a;seṣataḥ ‖
Manu10-26a/ sūto vaidehaka;s ca-eva caṇḍāla;s ca narādhamaḥ |
Manu10-26c/ māgadhaḥ  tathā-āyogava eva ca kṣatrajāti;s ca ‖ [ṃ-kṣattṛjāti;s ca]
Manu10-27a/ ete ṣaṭ sadṛ;sān varṇā;n janayanti) svayoniṣu |
Manu10-27c/ mātṛjātyāṃ prasūyante) pravārāsu ca yoniṣu ‖ [ṃ-mātṛjātyāḥ ]
Manu10-28a/ yathā trayāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ dvayor ātmā-asya jāyate) |
Manu10-28c/ ānantaryāt svayonyāṃ tu tathā bāhyeṣv api kramāt  ‖ [ṃ-kramaḥ]
Manu10-29a/ te ca-api bāhyān subahūṃs tato 'apy adhikadūṣitān |
Manu10-29c/ parasparasya dāreṣu janayanti) vigarhitān ‖
Manu10-30a/ yathā-eva ;sūdro brāhmaṇyāṃ bāhyaṃ jantuṃ prasūyate) |
Manu10-30c/ tathā bāhyataraṃ bāhya;s cāturvarṇye prasūyate) ‖
Manu10-31a/ pratikūlaṃ vartamānā) bāhyā bāhyatarān punaḥ |
Manu10-31c/ hīnā hīnān prasūyante) varṇān pa;ncada;sa-eva tu ‖

Manu10-32a/ prasādhana-upacāraj;nam adāsaṃ dāsajīvanam | [ṃ-dāsyajīvinam ]
Manu10-32c/ sairindhraṃ vāgurā-vṛttiṃ sūte) dasyur ayogave ‖ [ṃ- sairandhraṃ ]
Manu10-33a/ maitreyakaṃ tu vaideho mādhūkaṃ saṃprasūyate) | 
Manu10-33c/ nṝn pra;saṃsaty) ajasraṃ yo ghaṇṭātāḍo 'aruṇa-udaye ‖
Manu10-34a/ niṣādo mārgavaṃ sūte) dāsaṃ naukarmajīvinam | 
Manu10-34c/ kaivartam iti yaṃ prāhur) āryāvartanivāsinaḥ ‖
Manu10-35a/ mṛtavastrabhṛtsv nārīṣu garhita-anna-a;sanāsu ca |[ṃ-anāryāṣu]
Manu10-35c/ bhavanty) āyogavīṣv ete jātihīnāḥ pṛthak trayaḥ ‖
Manu10-36a/ kārāvaro niṣādāt tu carmakāraḥ prasūyate) | [ṃ-carmakāraṃ ]
Manu10-36c/ vaidehikād andhra-medau bahirgrāma-prati;srayau ‖

Manu10-39c/ ;sma;sāna-gocaraṃ sūte) bāhyānām api garhitam ‖
Manu10-40a/ saṃkare jātayas tv etāḥ pitṛ-mātṛ-pradar;sitāḥ |
Manu10-40c/ prachannā vā prakā;sā vā veditavyāḥ svakarmabhiḥ ‖
Manu10-41a/ svajātija-anantarajāḥ ṣaṭ sutā dvijadharmiṇaḥ |
Manu10-41c/ ;sūdrāṇāṃ tu sa-dharmāṇaḥ sarve 'apadhvaṃsajāḥ smṛtāḥ) ‖
Manu10-42a/ tapo-bīja-prabhāvais tu te gacchanti) yuge yuge |
Manu10-42c/ utkarṣaṃ ca-apakarṣaṃ ca manuṣyeṣv iha janmataḥ ‖
Manu10-43a/ ;sanakais tu kriyālopād imāḥ kṣatriya-jātayaḥ |
Manu10-43c/ vṛṣalatvaṃ gatā) loke brāhmaṇādar;sanena ca ‖ [ṃ-brāhmaṇātikrameṇa ca]
Manu10-44a/ pauṇḍrakā;s cauḍra-draviḍāḥ  kāmbojā yavanāḥ ;sakāḥ | [ṃ-puṇḍrakā;s coḍa-draviḍāḥ]
Manu10-44c/ pāradā-pahlavā;s cīnāḥ kirātā daradāḥ kha;sāḥ ‖ 
Manu10-45a/ mukha-bāhu-ūru-pad-jānāṃ yā loke jātayo bahiḥ |
Manu10-45c/ mleccha-vāca;s ca-ārya-vācaḥ sarve te dasyavaḥ smṛtāḥ) ‖
Manu10-46a/ ye dvijānām apasadā ye ca-apadhvaṃsajāḥ smṛtāḥ) |
Manu10-46c/ te ninditair vartayeyur) dvijānām eva karmabhiḥ ‖
Manu10-47a/ sūtānām a;sva-sārathyam ambaṣṭhānāṃ cikitsanam |
Manu10-47c/ vaidehakānāṃ strīkāryaṃ māgadhānāṃ vaṇikpathaḥ ‖
Manu10-48a/ matsyaghāto niṣādānāṃ tvaṣṭis tv āyogavasya ca | 
Manu10-48c/ meda-andhra-cu;ncu-madgūnām āraṇyapa;suhiṃsanam ‖
Manu10-49a/ kṣattṛ-ugra-pukkasānāṃ tu bilaukovadha-bandhanam |
Manu10-49c/ dhigvaṇānāṃ carmakāryaṃ veṇānāṃ bhāṇḍavādanam ‖
Manu10-50a/ caityadruma-;sma;sāneṣu ;saileṣu-upavaneṣu ca |
Manu10-50c/ vaseyur) ete vij;nātā vartayantaḥ) svakarmabhiḥ ‖
Manu10-51a/ caṇḍāla-;svapacānāṃ tu bahir grāmāt prati;srayaḥ |
Manu10-51c/ apapātrā;s ca kartavyā) dhanam eṣāṃ ;sva-gardabham ‖
Manu10-52a/ vāsāṃsi mṛtacailāni bhinnabhāṇḍeṣu bhojanam |
Manu10-52c/ kārṣṇāyasam ala:nkāraḥ parivrajyā ca nitya;saḥ ‖
Manu10-53a/ na taiḥ samayam anvicchet) puruṣo dharmam ācaran) |

Manu10-55c/ a-bāndhavaṃ ;savaṃ ca-eva nirhareyur) iti sthitiḥ ‖

Manu10-59a/ pitryaṃ vā bhajate) ;sīlaṃ mātur vā-ubhayam eva vā |
Manu10-59c/ na kathaṃ cana duryoniḥ prakṛtiṃ svāṃ niyacchati) ‖
Manu10-60a/ kule mukhye 'api jātasya yasya syād) yonisaṃkaraḥ |
Manu10-60c/ saṃ;srayaty) eva tat-;sīlaṃ naro 'alpam api vā bahu ‖
Manu10-61a/ yatra tv ete paridhvaṃsāj jāyante) varṇadūṣakāḥ |
Manu10-61c/ rāṣṭrikaiḥ saha tad rāṣṭraṃ kṣipram eva vina;syati) ‖ [ṃ-rāṣṭriyaiḥ ]
Manu10-62a/ brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā dehatyāgo 'an-upaskṛtaḥ |
Manu10-62c/ strī-bālābhyupapattau ca bāhyānāṃ siddhikāraṇam ‖ [ṃ-strī-bālābhyavapattau ca ]

Manu10-63c/ etaṃ sāmāsikaṃ dharmaṃ cāturvarṇye 'abravīn) manuḥ ‖

Manu10-64a/ ;sūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jātaḥ ;sreyasā cet prajāyate) |
Manu10-64c/ a;sreyān ;sreyasīṃ jātiṃ gacchaty) ā saptamād yugāt ‖

Manu10-65c/ kṣatriyāj jātam evaṃ tu vidyād) vai;syāt tathā-eva ca ‖
Manu10-66a/ anāryāyāṃ samutpanno brāhmaṇāt tu yadṛcchayā |

Manu10-68a/ tāv ubhāv apy asaṃskāryāv iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ |
Manu10-68c/ vaiguṇyāj janmanaḥ pūrva uttaraḥ pratilomataḥ ‖ [ṃ-janmataḥ ]

Manu10-70a/ bījam eke pra;saṃsanti) kṣetram anye manīṣiṇaḥ |
Manu10-70c/ bīja-kṣetre tathā-eva-anye tatra-iyaṃ tu vyavasthitiḥ ‖

Manu10-71c/ a-bījakam api kṣetraṃ kevalaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ bhavet) |
Manu10-72a/ yasmād bījaprabhāveṇa tiryagjā ṛṣayo 'abhavan) |
Manu10-72c/ pūjitā;s ca pra;sastā;s ca tasmād bījaṃ pra;sasyate ‖ [ṃ-vi;siṣyate)]
Manu10-73a/ anāryam ārya-karmāṇam āryaṃ ca-anāryakarmiṇam |
Manu10-73c/ saṃpradhārya)-abravīd) dhātā na samau na-asamāv iti ‖

Manu10-74a/ brāhmaṇā brahmayonisthā ye svakarmaṇy avasthitāḥ) |
Manu10-74c/ te samyag upajīveyuḥ) ṣaṭ karmāṇi yathākramam ‖
Manu10-75a/ adhyāpanam adhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ yājanaṃ tathā |

Manu10-76a/ ṣaṇṇāṃ tu karmaṇām asya trīṇi karmāṇi jīvikā |
Manu10-76c/ yājana-adhyāpane ca-eva vi;suddhāc ca pratigrahaḥ ‖
Manu10-77a/ trayo dharmā nivartante) brāhmaṇāt kṣatriyaṃ prati |
Manu10-77c/ adhyāpanaṃ yājanaṃ ca tṛtīya;s ca pratigrahaḥ ‖
Manu10-78a/ vai;syaṃ prati tathā-eva-ete nivarterann) iti sthitiḥ |
Manu10-78c/ na tau prati hi tān dharmān manur āha) prajāpatiḥ ‖ [ṃ- prati hitān dharmān ]
Manu10-79a/ ;sastra-astrabhṛttvaṃ kṣatrasya vaṇik-pa;su-kṛṣir viṣaḥ |

Manu10-81a/ a-jīvaṃs) tu yathā-uktena brāhmaṇaḥ svena karmaṇā |

Manu10-86c/ a;smano lavaṇaṃ ca-eva pa;savo ye ca mānuṣāḥ ‖

Manu10-88a/ apaḥ ;sastraṃ viṣaṃ māṃsaṃ somaṃ gandhāṃ;s ca sarva;saḥ |

Manu10-95a/ jīved) etena rājanyaḥ sarveṇa-apy anayaṃ gataḥ |

Manu10-96c/ taṃ rājā nirdhanaṃ kṛtvā) kṣipram eva pravāsayet) ‖

Manu10-118c/ prajā rakṣan) paraṃ ;saktyā kilbiṣāt pratimucyate) ‖
Manu10-119a/ svadharmo vijayas tasya na-āhave syāt parā:n-mukhaḥ |
Manu10-119c/ ;sastreṇa vai;syān rakṣitvā) dharmyam āhārayed) balim ‖ [ṃ-vai;syād rakṣitvā ]
Manu10-120a/ dhānye 'aṣṭamaṃ vi;sāṃ ;sulkaṃ viṃ;saṃ kārṣāpaṇa-avaram |
Manu10-120c/ karma-upakaraṇāḥ ;sūdrāḥ kāravaḥ ;silpinas tathā ‖

Manu11-255a[254ṃa]/ abdārdham indram ity etad enasvī saptakaṃ japet) | 
Manu11-255c[254ṃc]/ apra;sastaṃ tu kṛtvā)-apsu māsam āsīta) bhaikṣabhuk ‖
Manu11-256a[255ṃa]/ mantraiḥ ;sākalahomīyair abdaṃ hutvā) ghṛtaṃ dvijaḥ |
Manu11-256c[255ṃc]/ sugurv apy apahanty) eno japtvā) vā nama ity ṛcam ‖
Manu11-257a[256ṃa]/ mahāpātakasaṃyukto 'anugacched) gāḥ samāhitaḥ |
Manu11-257c[256ṃc]/ abhyasya)-abdaṃ pāvamānīr bhaikṣa-āhāro vi;sudhyati) ‖
Manu11-258a[257ṃa]/ araṇye vā trir abhyasya) prayato vedasaṃhitām |
Manu11-258c[257ṃc]/ mucyate) pātakaiḥ sarvaiḥ parākaiḥ ;sodhitas tribhiḥ ‖
Manu11-259a[258ṃa]/ tryahaṃ tu-upavased) yuktas trir ahno 'abhyupayann) apaḥ |
Manu11-259c[258ṃc]/ mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvais trir japitvā-aghamarṣaṇam ‖
Manu11-260a[259ṃa]/ yathā-a;svamedhaḥ kraturāj-sarvapāpāpa-nodanaḥ |
Manu11-260c[259ṃc]/ tathā-aghamarṣaṇaṃ sūktaṃ sarvapāpāpa-nodanam ‖
Manu11-261a[260ṃa]/ hatvā) lokān api-imāṃs trīn a;snann) api yatas tataḥ |
Manu11-261c[260ṃc]/ ṛgvedaṃ dhārayan) vipro na-enaḥ prāpnoti) kiṃ cana ‖
Manu11-262a[261ṃa]/ ṛksaṃhitāṃ trir abhyasya) yajuṣāṃ vā samāhitaḥ |
Manu11-262c[261ṃc]/ sāmnāṃ vā sa-rahasyānāṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate) ‖
Manu11-263a[262ṃa]/ yathā mahāhradaṃ prāpya) kṣiptaṃ loṣṭaṃ vina;syati) |
Manu11-263c[262ṃc]/ tathā du;scaritaṃ sarvaṃ vede trivṛti majjati) ‖
Manu11-264a[263ṃa]/ ṛco yajūṃṣi ca-anyāni sāmāni vividhāni ca |
Manu11-264c[263ṃc]/ eṣa j;neyas trivṛdvedo yo veda)-enaṃ sa vedavit ‖
Manu11-265a[264ṃa]/ ādyaṃ yat tryakṣaraṃ brahma trayī yasmin pratiṣṭhitā) |
Manu11-265c[264ṃc]/ sa guhyo 'anyas trivṛdvedo yas taṃ veda) sa vedavit ‖

<B 12>

Manu12-01a/ cāturvarṇyasya kṛtsno 'ayam ukto dharmas tvayā-anaghaḥ |
Manu12-01c/ karmaṇāṃ phalanirvṛttiṃ ;saṃsa) nas tattvataḥ parām ‖
Manu12-02a/ sa tān uvāca) dharma-ātmā maharṣīn mānavo bhṛguḥ |
Manu12-02c/ asya sarvasya ;sṛṇuta) karmayogasya nirṇayam ‖
Manu12-03a/ ;subha-a;subha-phalaṃ karma mano-vāc-deha-saṃbhavam |
Manu12-03c/ karmajā gatayo nṝṇām uttama-adhama-madhyamaḥ ‖

Manu12-07c/ paradāra-upasevā ca ;sārīraṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam ‖

Manu12-12a/ yo 'asya-ātmanaḥ kārayitā taṃ kṣetraj;naṃ pracakṣate) |

Manu12-13a/ jīvasaṃj;no 'antarātmā-anyaḥ sahajaḥ sarvadehinām |

Manu12-14a/ tāv ubhau bhūtasaṃpṛktau mahān kṣetraj;na eva ca |
Manu12-14c/ uccāvaceṣu bhūteṣu sthitaṃ taṃ vyāpya) tiṣṭhataḥ) ‖
Manu12-15a/ a-saṃkhyā mūrtayas tasya niṣpatanti) ;sarīrataḥ |

Manu12-16a/ pa;ncabhya eva mātrābhyaḥ pretya duṣkṛtināṃ nṛṇām | [ṃ-pa;ncabhya eva bhūtebhyaḥ ]
Manu12-16c/ ;sarīraṃ yātanārthīyam anyad utpadyate) dhruvam ‖
Manu12-17a/ tena-anubhūya) tā yāmīḥ ;sarīreṇa-iha yātanāḥ |

Manu12-85c/ tadd hy agryaṃ sarvavidyānāṃ prāpyate) hy amṛtaṃ tataḥ ‖
Manu12-86a/ ṣaṇṇām eṣāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ pretya ca-iha ca |
Manu12-86c/ ;sreyaskarataraṃ j;neyaṃ sarvadā karma vaidikam ‖
Manu12-87a/ vaidike karmayoge tu sarvāṇy etāny a;seṣataḥ |
Manu12-87c/ antarbhavanti) krama;sas tasmiṃs tasmin kriyāvidhau ‖
Manu12-88a/ sukhābhyudayikaṃ ca-eva naiḥ;sreyasikam eva ca |
Manu12-88c/ pravṛttaṃ ca nivṛttaṃ ca dvividhaṃ karma vaidikam ‖
Manu12-89a/ iha ca-amutra vā kāmyaṃ pravṛttaṃ karma kīrtyate) |
Manu12-89c/ niṣ-kāmaṃ j;nātapūrvaṃ tu nivṛttam upadi;syate) ‖
Manu12-90a/ pravṛttaṃ karma saṃsevyaṃ devānām eti) sāmyatām |
Manu12-90c/ nivṛttaṃ sevamānas) tu bhūtāny atyeti) pa;nca vai ‖

Manu12-91a/ sarvabhūteṣu ca-ātmānaṃ sarvabhūtāni ca-ātmani |
Manu12-91c/ samaṃ pa;syann) ātmayājī svārājyam adhigacchati) ‖
Manu12-92a/ yathā-uktāny api karmāṇi parihāya) dvijottamaḥ |
Manu12-92c/ ātmaj;nāne ;same ca syād) vedābhyāse ca yatnavān ‖
Manu12-93a/ etadd hi janmasāphalyaṃ brāhmaṇasya vi;seṣataḥ |
Manu12-93c/ prāpya)-etat kṛta-kṛtyo hi dvijo bhavati) na-anyathā ‖

Manu12-94a/ pitṛ-deva-manuṣyāṇāṃ veda;s cakṣuḥ sanātanam |
Manu12-94c/ a;sakyaṃ ca-aprameyaṃ ca veda;sāstram iti sthitiḥ ‖
Manu12-95a/ yā vedabāhyāḥ smṛtayo yā;s ca kā;s ca kudṛṣṭayaḥ | [ṃ-;srutayo ]
Manu12-95c/ sarvās tā niṣ-phalāḥ pretya tamo-niṣṭhā hi tāḥ smṛtāḥ ‖ 
Manu12-96a/ utpadyante) cyavante) ca yāny ato 'anyāni kāni cit | [ṃ-utpadyante) vina;syanti) ]
Manu12-96c/ tāny arvākkālikatayā niṣ-phalāny anṛtāni ca ‖
Manu12-97a/ cāturvarṇyaṃ trayo lokā;s catvāra;s ca-ā;sramāḥ pṛthak |
Manu12-97c/ bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ ca sarvaṃ vedāt prasidhyati) ‖ [ṃ-bhūtaṃ bhavad bhaviṣyaṃ ca ]
Manu12-98a/ ;sabdaḥ spar;sa;s ca rūpaṃ ca raso gandha;s ca pa;ncamaḥ |
Manu12-98c/ vedād eva prasūyante) prasūtir guṇa-karmataḥ ‖ 
Manu12-99a/ bibharti) sarvabhūtāni veda;sāstraṃ sanātanam |
Manu12-99c/ tasmād etat paraṃ manye) yat-jantor asya sādhanam ‖
Manu12-100a/ senāpatyaṃ ca rājyaṃ ca daṇḍanetṛtvam eva ca | [ṃ-saināpatyaṃ ]
Manu12-100c/ sarvalokādhipatyaṃ ca veda;sāstravid arhati) ‖
Manu12-101a/ yathā jāta-balo vahnir dahaty) ārdrān api drumān |
Manu12-101c/ tathā dahati) vedaj;naḥ karmajaṃ doṣam ātmanaḥ ‖
Manu12-102a/ veda;sāstrārthatattvaj;no yatra tatra-ā;srame vasan) |
Manu12-102c/ iha-eva loke tiṣṭhan) sa brahmabhūyāya kalpate) ‖
Manu12-103a/ aj;nebhyo granthinaḥ ;sreṣṭhā granthibhyo dhāriṇo varāḥ |
Manu12-103c/ dhāribhyo j;nāninaḥ ;sreṣṭhā j;nānibhyo vyavasāyinaḥ ‖
Manu12-104a/ tapo vidyā ca viprasya niḥ;sreyasakaraṃ param |
Manu12-104c/ tapasā kilbiṣaṃ hanti) vidyayā-amṛtam a;snute) ‖
Manu12-105a/ pratyakṣaṃ ca-anumānaṃ ca ;sāstraṃ ca vividhā-āgamam |
Manu12-105c/ trayaṃ suviditaṃ kāryaṃ dharma;suddhim abhīpsatā ‖
Manu12-106a/ ārṣaṃ dharma-upade;saṃ ca veda;sāstra-avirodhinā |
Manu12-106c/ yas tarkeṇa-anusaṃdhatte) sa dharmaṃ veda) na-itaraḥ ‖
Manu12-107a/ naiḥ;sreyasam idaṃ karma yathā-uditam a;seṣataḥ |
Manu12-107c/ mānavasya-asya ;sāstrasya rahasyam upadi;syate) ‖ [ṃ-upadekṣyate ]

Manu12-108a/ an-āmnāteṣu dharmeṣu kathaṃ syād) iti ced bhavet) |
Manu12-108c/ yaṃ ;siṣṭā brāhmaṇā brūyuḥ) sa dharmaḥ syād) a;sa:nkitaḥ ‖
Manu12-109a/ dharmeṇa-adhigato yais tu vedaḥ sa-paribṛṃhaṇaḥ |
Manu12-109c/ te ;siṣṭā brāhmaṇā j;neyāḥ ;sruti-pratyakṣahetavaḥ ‖
Manu12-110a/ da;sa-avarā vā pariṣadyaṃ dharmaṃ parikalpayet) |
Manu12-110c/ try-avarā vā-api vṛttasthā taṃ dharmaṃ na vicālayet) ‖
Manu12-111a/ traividyo hetukas tarkī nairukto dharmapāṭhakaḥ |
Manu12-111c/ traya;s ca-ā;sramiṇaḥ pūrve pariṣat syād) da;sa-avarā ‖
Manu12-112a/ ṛgvedavid yajurvid- ca sāmavedavid eva ca |
Manu12-112c/ try-avarā pariṣad- j;neyā dharmasaṃ;sayanirṇaye ‖
Manu12-113a/ eko 'api vedavid dharmaṃ yaṃ vyavasyed) dvijottamaḥ |
Manu12-113c/ sa vij;neyaḥ paro dharmo na-aj;nānām udito 'ayutaiḥ ‖
Manu12-114a/ a-vratānām a-mantrāṇāṃ jātimātra-upajīvinām |
Manu12-114c/ sahasra;saḥ sametānāṃ pariṣattvaṃ na vidyate) ‖
Manu12-115a/ yaṃ vadanti) tamobhūtā mūrkhā dharmam atadvidaḥ |
Manu12-115c/ tatpāpaṃ ;satadhā bhūtvā) tadvaktṝn anugacchati) ‖
Manu12-116a/ etad vo 'abhihitaṃ sarvaṃ niḥ;sreyasakaraṃ param |
Manu12-116c/ asmād apracyuto vipraḥ prāpnoti) paramāṃ gatim ‖

Manu12-117a/ evaṃ sa bhagavān devo lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā |
Manu12-117c/ dharmasya paramaṃ guhyaṃ mama-idaṃ sarvam uktavān) ‖
Manu12-118a/ sarvam ātmani saṃpa;syet) sat-ca-asat-ca samāhitaḥ |
Manu12-118c/ sarvaṃ hy ātmani saṃpa;syan) na-adharme kurute) manaḥ ‖ [ṃ-matim ]
Manu12-119a/ ātmā-eva devatāḥ sarvāḥ sarvam ātmany avasthitam |
Manu12-119c/ ātmā hi janayaty) eṣāṃ karmayogaṃ ;sarīriṇām ‖
Manu12-120a/ khaṃ saṃnive;sayet) kheṣu ceṣṭana-spar;sane 'anilam |
Manu12-120c/ pakti-dṛṣṭyoḥ paraṃ tejaḥ snehe 'apo gāṃ ca mūrtiṣu ‖
Manu12-121a/ manasi-induṃ di;saḥ ;srotre krānte viṣṇuṃ bale haram |
Manu12-121c/ vācy agniṃ mitram utsarge prajane ca prajāpatim ‖
Manu12-122a/ pra;sāsitāraṃ sarveṣām aṇīyāṃsam aṇor api |
Manu12-122c/ rukma-ābhaṃ svapnadhīgamyaṃ vidyāt) taṃ puruṣaṃ param ‖
Manu12-123a/ etam eke vadanty) agniṃ manum anye prajāpatim |
Manu12-123c/ indram eke pare prāṇam apare brahma ;sā;svatam ‖
Manu12-124a/ eṣa sarvāṇi bhūtāni pa;ncabhir vyāpya) mūrtibhiḥ |
Manu12-124c/ janma-vṛddhi-kṣayair nityaṃ saṃsārayati) cakravat ‖
Manu12-125a/ evaṃ yaḥ sarvabhūteṣu pa;syaty) ātmānam ātmanā |
Manu12-125c/ sa sarvasamatām etya) brahma-abhyeti) paraṃ padam |
Manu12-126a/ ity etan mānavaṃ ;sāstraṃ bhṛguproktaṃ paṭhan) dvijaḥ |
Manu12-126c/ bhavaty) ācāravān nityaṃ yathā-iṣṭāṃ prāpnuyād) gatim ‖

[samāptaṃ mānavaṃ dharma;sāstram]
